# Dogfight v2 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Dogfight'ı v2'ye taşımak: daha çok füze + yenilenme, dört harita (ada/şehir/çöl/dağ) ve üsler, pist/hangar kalkışı, iniş takımı ve inişte ikmal, hava durumu ve rüzgâr, üs saldırısı modu (bomba, yapılar, uçaksavar), oda listesi + Hızlı Oyna, hızlı sohbet, kalıcı pilot istatistikleri ve liderlik tablosu, metrikler, mobil dokunmatik kontrol, füze kamerası, killcam/izleyici/tekrar.

**Architecture:** v1 mimarisi korunur: deterministik `internal/sim` oda actor'ında 60 Hz koşar; client kendi uçağını tahmin eder. Yeni zemin modeli `StepFlight` içinde, Go ve TS'te aynı formüllerle; ortak vektörler (`testdata/vectors/*.json`) eşliği sabitler. Harita, üs ve bina üretimi yalnız Go'da (`internal/maps`), client welcome'dan hazır veriyi alır. Kalıcılık ve metrikler standart kütüphaneyle, actor/atomic ile yazılır; global durum yok.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.26, `github.com/coder/websocket` (tek dış bağımlılık); TypeScript 5, Three.js, esbuild; Node 22 (`node --test`, `--experimental-strip-types`), repo içi `.tools/node/bin`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-dogfight-v2-design.md` (v2) — v1 spec'i `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-dogfight-design.md` üzerine yazar. Spec'teki `[Kn]` atıfları spec §17 Kararlar listesidir.

## Global Constraints

- Go modülü `playground`, `go 1.26`. Tek dış Go bağımlılığı `github.com/coder/websocket`; yeni `require` YASAK (SQLite, Prometheus client vb. yok).
- Client runtime bağımlılığı yalnız `three`; dev: `esbuild`, `typescript`, `@types/three`, `@types/node`. Yeni paket YASAK.
- Her kaynak dosya ≤ ~300 satır; plan bölmeleri baştan yapar. Bir dosya 300'ü geçecekse aynı task içinde böl.
- `internal/sim`, `internal/maps`, `internal/terrain`, `internal/weather` I/O, `time`, global rastgelelik kullanmaz; rastgelelik seed'li splitmix'ten gelir.
- Global değişken yok (sabit tablolar hariç). Paylaşılan durum yok; oda ve istatistik deposu actor; özetler `atomic` ile.
- Birimler metre/saniye/radyan, `Dt = 1/60`, Y yukarı, burun yerel −Z. Heading ψ: `fwd = (−sin ψ, 0, −cos ψ)`.
- v1 uçuş vektör senaryolarının (ilk 10) checkpoint değerleri DEĞİŞMEZ. Vektör dosyası yeniden üretildiğinde Task 11'deki karşılaştırma betiği geçmelidir.
- Kullanıcıya görünen metinler Türkçe; kod, tanımlayıcı ve yorumlar İngilizce.
- DOM'a metin yalnız `h()`/`text()` ile (text node); `innerHTML` YASAK. CSP değişmez (`connect-src 'self'` fetch'i kapsar).
- Güvenlik: yeni HTTP uç noktaları rate-limit'li, JSON, `no-store`; pilot token'ı ham hâliyle log/metrik/URL/diske asla yazılmaz.
- Go doğrulaması (her Go task'ı sonunda): `go build ./... && go vet ./... && go test ./... -race -short`
- Client doğrulaması (her client task'ı sonunda, repo kökünden): `export PATH="$PWD/.tools/node/bin:$PATH"; cd client && npm run check && npm test && npm run build`
- Commit: yalnız task'ın dosyaları açıkça `git add <yol>…` ile; `git add -A` / `git add .` YASAK; `rm -rf` YASAK. Commit komutu `.tools/gc -m "<mesaj>"` (iş saati dışı tarih damgası). Mesaj sonuna oturumun verdiği `Co-Authored-By` / `Claude-Session` satırları eklenir (git log'daki son commit'lerle aynı biçim).
- Bir task'ın testi başka bir task'ın henüz yazılmamış kodunu gerektirmez; task sırası bağımlılık sırasıdır.

## Review Focus

Spec'in ima ettiği ama tek bir task'ın asıl testinin doğrudan hedeflemediği, kullanıcıyı en çok ısıracak beş durum (her birinin testi sahibi task'a eklendi):

1. **Hangarda park hâlindeyken yeniden bağlanma / aynı tarafa iki oyuncu:** iki oyuncu aynı hangara doğmamalı; ayrılan oyuncunun hangarı hemen boşalmalı (Task 13: `TestRunwaySpawnParksInHangars`, `TestLeftPlaneFreesHangar`).
2. **Pist sonundan çimene taşma / taksi yolunda hızlı gitme:** 30 m/s üstü taksi yolunda ve yüzeysiz zeminde ölüm, altında yaşam (Task 14: `TestRollOffRunwayEndCrashes`, `TestTaxiwaySpeedLimit`).
3. **Yarım yazılmış istatistik günlüğü (elektrik kesintisi):** sunucu açılmalı, bozuk son satır atlanmalı, önceki kayıtlar kaybolmamalı, çift sayım olmamalı (Task 46: `TestRecoverTruncatedJournal`, `TestNoDoubleCountAfterCrashBetweenRenameAndTruncate`).
4. **Kötü niyetli `X-Pilot-Token` (çok uzun, yanlış alfabe, yok):** 404, hash hesaplanmadan, 8 KB üstü başlıkta bile hızlı (Task 48: `TestMeRejectsBadTokens`).
5. **Hızlı Oyna sırasında oda kapanması/dolması yarışı:** seçilen oda kapanırsa ya da dolarsa oyuncu hata almaz, yeni oda kurulur (Task 43: `TestQuickFallsBackWhenPickedRoomFills`).

`types.go` `Plane`'e (`OutOfBounds`'tan sonra):


`regen.go`:

```go
package sim

const (
	MissileRegenTicks = 20 * 60 // +1 missile every 20 s below the loadout
	FlareRegenTicks   = 10 * 60 // +1 flare every 10 s below the loadout
)

// regen adds one to *count every `every` ticks while it is below limit. The
// timer starts on the first tick the count is seen below the limit and
// idles (0) once full.
func regen(count *int, limit int, at *int, tick, every int) {
	switch {
	case *count >= limit:
		*at = 0
	case *at == 0:
		*at = tick + every
	case tick >= *at:
		*count++
		*at = 0
		if *count < limit {
			*at = tick + every
		}
	}
}

func (w *World) regenAmmo(p *Plane) {
	s := SpecOf(p.Kind)
	regen(&p.Missiles, s.Missiles, &p.MissileRegenAt, w.tick, MissileRegenTicks)
	regen(&p.Flares, s.Flares, &p.FlareRegenAt, w.tick, FlareRegenTicks)
}
```

`world.go` `Step` içinde `w.dropFlare(p, in, &evs)` satırının hemen altına `w.regenAmmo(p)`. `world_test.go:29` `a.Missiles != 2` → `a.Missiles != 4`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `world.go` `Config`:

```go
	LockRangeMul float64 // weather scale on every lock range; 0 means 1
```

`missile.go`:

```go
// lockRange is the aircraft's lock range scaled by the weather.
func (w *World) lockRange(k Kind) float64 {
	m := w.cfg.LockRangeMul
	if m <= 0 {
		m = 1
	}
	return SpecOf(k).LockRange * m
}
```

`updateLock` içinde `lockRange := SpecOf(p.Kind).LockRange` → `lockRange := w.lockRange(p.Kind)`. `fireMissile` olayı: `Event{Kind: EvMissileLaunch, Plane: m.ID, Other: m.Target, Pos: m.Pos, By: p.ID}`. `types.go` `Event`'e `By ID // owner (missile launch, bombs)`. `events.go`: `EventJSON`'a `O sim.ID \`json:"o,omitempty"\`` ve `NewEvents`'te `j.O = e.By`. `protocol.ts` `EventJSON` tipine `o?: number; // owner (mlaunch, bdrop, boom)`.

**Files:**

Dışarıdaki her eşleşme için, kazanma oranı yüksek olan uçağın `LockRange`'ini 50 m düşür (ya da düşük olanınkini 50 m artır); `LockRange` değişikliği yeterli değilse aynı uçağın `PitchRate`'ini 0.04 düşür/artır. - [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `Generate`'in döngüsünü kelimesi kelimesine `IslandRaw`'a taşı (`x := NodeCoord(xi)`, `z := NodeCoord(zi)` ile; `-Size/2 + float64(xi)*cell` ifadesiyle aynı sonucu verir), sonra:

```go
const Cell = Size / (Res - 1)

const cell = Cell

func NodeCoord(i int) float64 { return -Size/2 + float64(i)*Cell }

// FromRaw quantizes a raw height grid exactly like the wire format, so the
// server's map equals the client's decoded one.
func FromRaw(raw []float64) *Map {
	m, err := Decode((&Map{h: raw}).Encode())
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) // programming error: wrong grid size
	}
	return m
}

func Generate(seed int64) *Map { return FromRaw(IslandRaw(seed)) }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `internal/rng/rng.go`:

```go
// Package rng is seeded splitmix64 for deterministic map and weather generation.
package rng

const golden = 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15

func mix(z uint64) uint64 {
	z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9
	z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EB
	return z ^ (z >> 31)
}

func unit(z uint64) float64 { return float64(z>>11) / float64(1<<53) }

// Mix is the i-th value of seed's sequence, without state.
func Mix(seed int64, i uint64) float64 { return unit(mix(uint64(seed) + (i+1)*golden)) }

type Stream struct{ s uint64 }

func New(seed int64, salt uint64) *Stream { return &Stream{s: uint64(seed) ^ salt} }

func (r *Stream) Float() float64 {
	r.s += golden
	return unit(mix(r.s))
}
```

Sonra `biomes.go` — formüller spec §4.1'den birebir; her üreteç `n := noise{seed: uint64(seed)*0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 + K}` (K: şehir 2, çöl 3, dağ 4) kullanır ve `grid(func(x, z float64) float64)` yardımcısıyla doldurur:

```go
package terrain

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/rng"
)

// grid fills a Res×Res raw height grid from f(x, z) at the node coordinates.
func grid(f func(x, z float64) float64) []float64 {
	raw := make([]float64, Res*Res)
	for zi := range Res {
		for xi := range Res {
			raw[zi*Res+xi] = f(NodeCoord(xi), NodeCoord(zi))
		}
	}
	return raw
}

func CityRaw(seed int64) []float64 {
	n := noise{seed: uint64(seed)*0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 + 2}
	return grid(func(x, z float64) float64 {
		plain := 12 + 18*n.fbm(x/2500, z/2500, 3)
		hills := math.Pow(n.fbm(x/1200+11, z/1200-5, 4), 2.2) * 700 * smoothstep(-2000, -3800, z)
		coast := smoothstep(2600, 3600, z)
		return (plain+hills)*(1-coast) - 40*coast
	})
}

func DesertRaw(seed int64) []float64 {
	n := noise{seed: uint64(seed)*0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 + 3}
	ox, oz := (rng.Mix(seed, 0)-0.5)*1500, (rng.Mix(seed, 1)-0.5)*1500
	return grid(func(x, z float64) float64 {
		dunes := 25 * (0.5 + 0.5*math.Sin((0.8*x+0.6*z)/180+3*n.fbm(x/900, z/900, 2)))
		mesa := smoothstep(0.58, 0.62, n.fbm(x/1600+7, z/1600-3, 4)) * (220 + 120*n.fbm(x/500, z/500, 2))
		c := math.Abs(2*n.fbm(x/1100-9, z/1100+4, 3) - 1)
		canyon := (1 - smoothstep(0, 0.06, c)) * 90
		h := math.Max(6, 40+dunes+mesa-canyon)
		o := math.Hypot(x-ox, z-oz)
		return math.Min(h, -6+(h+6)*smoothstep(120, 260, o))
	})
}

func MountainRaw(seed int64) []float64 {
	n := noise{seed: uint64(seed)*0x9E3779B97F4A7C15 + 4}
	return grid(func(x, z float64) float64 {
		ridge := 1 - math.Abs(2*n.fbm(x/1300+3, z/1300-8, 5)-1)
		mount := math.Pow(ridge, 2.2)*2000 + n.fbm(x/600, z/600, 3)*250
		vW := 1 - smoothstep(250, 900, math.Abs(x+2600))
		vE := 1 - smoothstep(250, 900, math.Abs(x-2600))
		vC := 0.6 * (1 - smoothstep(250, 900, math.Abs(z)))
		v := math.Max(vW, math.Max(vE, vC))
		return 60 + mount*(1-0.9*v)
	})
}
```

`smoothstep(a, b, x)` mevcut tanımla `a > b` iken de doğru çalışır (`t = (x−a)/(b−a)`). Test eşikleri (120 m) tutmazsa formülü DEĞİŞTİRME; spec'i yazanı uyar (BLOCKED + ölçülen değer).

```go
type Kind uint8
const (Ada Kind = iota + 1; Sehir; Col; Dag)
func ParseKind(s string) (Kind, bool) // "ada","sehir","col","dag"
func (k Kind) String() string

type Surface uint8
const (SurfNone Surface = iota; SurfRunway; SurfTaxi)

type Area struct{ MinX, MinZ, MaxX, MaxZ float64; Surf Surface }
func (a Area) Contains(x, z float64) bool

type Hangar struct{ Spawn geom.Vec3; Heading float64 } // Spawn.Y = paved elevation
type Base struct {
	Side    int        // 0 NATO, 1 Soviet
	Center  geom.Vec3  // runway center, Y = quantized elevation
	Axis    geom.Vec3  // unit along the runway (+u), world X or Z
	Inner   geom.Vec3  // unit toward the apron (+v)
	Areas   []Area     // [0] runway, then taxiway, connectors, apron, 6 hangar floors
	Hangars []Hangar   // 6, index i at u = -250 + 100*i
	Bounds  Area       // footprint u ±1000, v -80..360 (Surf = SurfNone)
}
func (b Base) World(u, v float64) geom.Vec3        // Y = Center.Y
func (b Base) Waypoints(hangar int) []geom.Vec3    // door, apron exit, taxi end, hold, lineup
func (b Base) Lineup() (geom.Vec3, float64)        // World(-700,0), HeadingOf(Axis)
func (b Base) Threshold() (geom.Vec3, float64)     // World(-850,0), HeadingOf(Axis)
func HeadingOf(d geom.Vec3) float64                // atan2(-d.X, -d.Z)

type Map struct {
	Kind    Kind
	Seed    int64
	Terrain *terrain.Map
	Bases   [2]Base
	// Buildings, Solids (Task 7), Structures (Task 25) are added later.
}
func Build(k Kind, seed int64) *Map
func (m *Map) SurfaceAt(x, z float64) Surface // runway wins over taxi
func (m *Map) BaseAt(x, z float64) int        // side whose Bounds contain (x,z), else -1
// layout constants (exported, the client and bots mirror them):
const (RunwayHalfL = 900.0; RunwayHalfW = 22.5; HangarCount = 6; HangarW = 40.0; HangarD = 30.0; HangarH = 12.0)
```

```go
var kinds = []Kind{Ada, Sehir, Col, Dag}

func TestParseKind(t *testing.T) {

			t.Fatalf("round trip %v", k)

		if !reflect.DeepEqual(a.Bases, b.Bases) || !reflect.DeepEqual(a.Terrain.Encode(), b.Terrain.Encode()) {

func TestBasesFlatPavedAndInBounds(t *testing.T) {
	for _, k := range kinds {
		m := Build(k, 1)
		for side, b := range m.Bases {
			if b.Side != side || b.Center.Y < 8 || b.Center.Y > 350 {

				{0, 170, SurfTaxi}, {-250, 225, SurfTaxi}, {0, 60, SurfNone}, {0, 300, SurfNone}, {950, 0, SurfNone}}
			for _, c := range cases {
				if got := at(c.u, c.v); got != c.want {
					t.Fatalf("%v base %d surface at (%v,%v) = %v, want %v", k, side, c.u, c.v, got, c.want)
				}

				t.Fatalf("hangars %d", len(b.Hangars))
			}
		}
		if m.BaseAt(0, 0) != -1 {
			t.Fatal("map center is no base")
		}
	}
}

	for _, b := range m.Bases {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`maps.go`: `Kind`, `ParseKind`, `String`, `Surface`, `Area.Contains` (`x >= MinX && x <= MaxX && z >= MinZ && z <= MaxZ`), `Map`, `SurfaceAt` (iki üssün `Areas`'ını tara; `SurfRunway` bulunursa hemen döndür, `SurfTaxi` görülürse hatırla), `BaseAt`, ve:

```go
// Build generates the map for (k, seed): raw terrain, two bases placed and
// flattened into it, then quantized. Everything is a pure function of (k, seed).
func Build(k Kind, seed int64) *Map {
	raw := rawFor(k, seed)
	m := &Map{Kind: k, Seed: seed}
	for side := range 2 {
		m.Bases[side] = placeBase(raw, nominals[k][side])
		flatten(raw, m.Bases[side])
	}
	m.Terrain = terrain.FromRaw(raw)
	return m
}

func rawFor(k Kind, seed int64) []float64 {
	switch k {
	case Sehir:
		return terrain.CityRaw(seed)
	case Col:
		return terrain.DesertRaw(seed)
	case Dag:
		return terrain.MountainRaw(seed)
	}
	return terrain.IslandRaw(seed)
}
```

`base.go`: spec §4.3 sabitleri ve düzen.

```go
const (
	RunwayHalfL, RunwayHalfW = 900.0, 22.5
	taxiV0, taxiV1, taxiHalfL = 110.0, 130.0, 710.0
	connU0, connU1           = 690.0, 710.0
	apronV0, apronV1, apronHalfL = 130.0, 210.0, 300.0
	HangarCount                = 6
	HangarPitch, HangarW, HangarD, HangarH = 100.0, 40.0, 30.0, 12.0
	hangarV0                   = 210.0
	footU, footV0, footV1      = 1000.0, -80.0, 360.0
)

type nominal struct {
	side          int
	x, z          float64
	axis, inner   geom.Vec3
}

var nominals = map[Kind][2]nominal{
	Ada:   {{0, -2400, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(1, 0, 0)}, {1, 2400, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(-1, 0, 0)}},
	Sehir: {{0, -2900, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(1, 0, 0)}, {1, 2900, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(-1, 0, 0)}},
	Col:   {{0, 0, -2600, geom.V(1, 0, 0), geom.V(0, 0, 1)}, {1, 0, 2600, geom.V(1, 0, 0), geom.V(0, 0, -1)}},
	Dag:   {{0, -2600, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(1, 0, 0)}, {1, 2600, 0, geom.V(0, 0, 1), geom.V(-1, 0, 0)}},
}

func HeadingOf(d geom.Vec3) float64 { return math.Atan2(-d.X, -d.Z) }

func (b Base) World(u, v float64) geom.Vec3 {
	p := b.Center.Add(b.Axis.Scale(u)).Add(b.Inner.Scale(v))
	p.Y = b.Center.Y
	return p
}

// area converts a local rect to a world AABB (axes are world-aligned).
func (b Base) area(u0, u1, v0, v1 float64, s Surface) Area {
	p, q := b.World(u0, v0), b.World(u1, v1)
	return Area{math.Min(p.X, q.X), math.Min(p.Z, q.Z), math.Max(p.X, q.X), math.Max(p.Z, q.Z), s}
}

// layout fills Areas, Hangars and Bounds for a base at center.
func (b *Base) layout() {
	b.Areas = []Area{
		b.area(-RunwayHalfL, RunwayHalfL, -RunwayHalfW, RunwayHalfW, SurfRunway),
		b.area(-taxiHalfL, taxiHalfL, taxiV0, taxiV1, SurfTaxi),
		b.area(-connU1, -connU0, RunwayHalfW, taxiV0, SurfTaxi),
		b.area(connU0, connU1, RunwayHalfW, taxiV0, SurfTaxi),
		b.area(-apronHalfL, apronHalfL, apronV0, apronV1, SurfTaxi),
	}
	b.Hangars = make([]Hangar, HangarCount)
	for i := range HangarCount {
		u := hangarU(i)
		b.Areas = append(b.Areas, b.area(u-HangarW/2, u+HangarW/2, hangarV0, hangarV0+HangarD, SurfTaxi))
		b.Hangars[i] = Hangar{Spawn: b.World(u, hangarV0+16), Heading: HeadingOf(b.Inner.Scale(-1))}
	}
	b.Bounds = b.area(-footU, footU, footV0, footV1, SurfNone)
}

func hangarU(i int) float64 { return -250 + HangarPitch*float64(i) }

func (b Base) Waypoints(i int) []geom.Vec3 {
	u := hangarU(i)
	return []geom.Vec3{b.World(u, 205), b.World(u, 120), b.World(-700, 120), b.World(-700, 40), b.World(-700, 0)}
}

func (b Base) Lineup() (geom.Vec3, float64)    { return b.World(-700, 0), HeadingOf(b.Axis) }
func (b Base) Threshold() (geom.Vec3, float64) { return b.World(-850, 0), HeadingOf(b.Axis) }
```

`flatten.go`:

```go
const (
	flattenMargin = 160.0
	blendWidth    = 400.0
	minElev, maxElev = 8.0, 350.0
)

// local returns (u, v) of world (x, z) in b's frame.
func (b Base) local(x, z float64) (float64, float64) {
	d := geom.V(x-b.Center.X, 0, z-b.Center.Z)
	return d.Dot(b.Axis), d.Dot(b.Inner)
}

// placeBase tries 25 centers around the nominal one and keeps the flattest
// (variance + 1000 per node below minElev); ties keep the first candidate.
func placeBase(raw []float64, n nominal) Base {
	var best Base
	bestCost := math.Inf(1)
	for _, dx := range []float64{-400, -200, 0, 200, 400} {
		for _, dz := range []float64{-400, -200, 0, 200, 400} {
			b := Base{Side: n.side, Center: geom.V(n.x+dx, 0, n.z+dz), Axis: n.axis, Inner: n.inner}
			mean, cost := footprintStats(raw, b)
			if cost < bestCost {
				b.Center.Y = quantize(math.Max(minElev, math.Min(maxElev, mean)))
				best, bestCost = b, cost
			}
		}
	}
	best.layout()
	return best
}

func footprintStats(raw []float64, b Base) (mean, cost float64) {
	var sum, sum2, n, low float64
	for zi := range terrain.Res {
		for xi := range terrain.Res {
			u, v := b.local(terrain.NodeCoord(xi), terrain.NodeCoord(zi))
			if math.Abs(u) > footU || v < footV0 || v > footV1 {
				continue
			}
			h := raw[zi*terrain.Res+xi]
			sum, sum2, n = sum+h, sum2+h*h, n+1
			if h < minElev {
				low++
			}
		}
	}
	mean = sum / n
	return mean, sum2/n - mean*mean + 1000*low
}

// quantize rounds h to the 0.1 m wire step, so the flattened grid decodes to exactly b.Center.Y.
func quantize(h float64) float64 { return math.Round((h+500)*10)/10 - 500 }

// flatten sets every node within the footprint plus margin to the base
// elevation and blends a ring of blendWidth back to the raw terrain.
func flatten(raw []float64, b Base) {
	for zi := range terrain.Res {
		for xi := range terrain.Res {
			u, v := b.local(terrain.NodeCoord(xi), terrain.NodeCoord(zi))
			du := math.Max(0, math.Abs(u)-(footU+flattenMargin))
			dv := math.Max(0, math.Max((footV0-flattenMargin)-v, v-(footV1+flattenMargin)))
			d := math.Hypot(du, dv)
			i := zi*terrain.Res + xi
			switch {
			case d == 0:
				raw[i] = b.Center.Y
			case d < blendWidth:
				raw[i] = b.Center.Y + (raw[i]-b.Center.Y)*smoothstep(0, blendWidth, d)
			}
		}
	}
}

func smoothstep(a, b, x float64) float64 {
	t := math.Max(0, math.Min(1, (x-a)/(b-a)))
	return t * t * (3 - 2*t)
}
```

Not: tüm pistlerde kalkış `+u` yönüne, iniş yaklaşması `−u` tarafından (eşikten 2.5 km) yapılır; nominal merkezler bu yaklaşma noktası ±4000 içinde kalacak şekilde seçildi (Z eksenli üsler merkez `z≈0`, X eksenli çöl üsleri merkez `x=0`).

`index.go`:

```go
const (cellSize = 100.0; cells = 100; origin = -5000.0; maxGrow = 10.0)

type Index struct {
	boxes []Box
	cells [cells * cells][]int32
}

func cellOf(v float64) int { return max(0, min(cells-1, int(math.Floor((v-origin)/cellSize)))) }

func NewIndex(boxes []Box) *Index {
	ix := &Index{boxes: boxes}
	for i, b := range boxes {
		for cz := cellOf(b.Min.Z - maxGrow); cz <= cellOf(b.Max.Z+maxGrow); cz++ {
			for cx := cellOf(b.Min.X - maxGrow); cx <= cellOf(b.Max.X+maxGrow); cx++ {
				ix.cells[cz*cells+cx] = append(ix.cells[cz*cells+cx], int32(i))
			}
		}
	}
	return ix
}

func (ix *Index) Boxes() []Box { return ix.boxes }

// Sweep: r must be <= maxGrow (cells were registered with that margin).
func (ix *Index) Sweep(a, b geom.Vec3, r float64) (float64, int, bool) {
	best, hit := math.Inf(1), -1
	for cz := cellOf(math.Min(a.Z, b.Z) - r); cz <= cellOf(math.Max(a.Z, b.Z)+r); cz++ {
		for cx := cellOf(math.Min(a.X, b.X) - r); cx <= cellOf(math.Max(a.X, b.X)+r); cx++ {
			for _, i := range ix.cells[cz*cells+cx] {
				bx := ix.boxes[i]
				g := geom.V(r, r, r)
				if t, ok := segBox(a, b.Sub(a), bx.Min.Sub(g), bx.Max.Add(g)); ok && (t < best || (t == best && int(i) < hit)) {
					best, hit = t, int(i)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return best, hit, hit >= 0
}

// segBox is the slab test for a + t·d, t ∈ [0,1], against [lo, hi].
func segBox(a, d, lo, hi geom.Vec3) (float64, bool) {
	t0, t1 := 0.0, 1.0
	for _, ax := range [3][4]float64{{a.X, d.X, lo.X, hi.X}, {a.Y, d.Y, lo.Y, hi.Y}, {a.Z, d.Z, lo.Z, hi.Z}} {
		p, dd, l, h := ax[0], ax[1], ax[2], ax[3]
		if dd == 0 {
			if p < l || p > h {
				return 0, false
			}
			continue
		}
		u, v := (l-p)/dd, (h-p)/dd
		if u > v {
			u, v = v, u
		}
		t0, t1 = math.Max(t0, u), math.Min(t1, v)
		if t0 > t1 {
			return 0, false
		}
	}
	return t0, true
}
```

`game.go`: `Settings`'e `Map maps.Kind`; `New` içinde `if s.Map == 0 { s.Map = maps.Ada }`, `m := maps.Build(s.Map, s.Seed)`, `t := m.Terrain`, `Config{…, Terrain: t, Map: m}`; `Game`'e `m *maps.Map` alanı ve `func (g *Game) Map() *maps.Map { return g.m }`.

`welcome.go`: taşınan kod + 

```go
type Static struct{ Terrain, Map json.RawMessage }

func NewStatic(g WelcomeSource) Static { return Static{Terrain: TerrainJSON(g), Map: MapJSON(g.Map())} }

// MapJSON encodes the static map layout; buildings to 0.1 m.
func MapJSON(m *maps.Map) json.RawMessage {
	info := MapInfo{Kind: m.Kind.String(), Bld: make([][6]float64, 0, len(m.Buildings))}
	for _, b := range m.Bases {
		info.Bases = append(info.Bases, baseJSON(b))
	}
	for _, b := range m.Buildings {
		info.Bld = append(info.Bld, [6]float64{round(b.Min.X, 10), round(b.Min.Y, 10), round(b.Min.Z, 10),
			round(b.Max.X, 10), round(b.Max.Y, 10), round(b.Max.Z, 10)})
	}
	out, err := json.Marshal(info)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) // plain numbers always marshal
	}
	return out
}

func baseJSON(b maps.Base) BaseJSON {
	j := BaseJSON{Side: TeamName(sim.Team(b.Side + 1)), C: r3(b.Center, 100),
		Axis: [2]float64{b.Axis.X, b.Axis.Z}, Inner: [2]float64{b.Inner.X, b.Inner.Z}}
	for _, a := range b.Areas {
		j.Areas = append(j.Areas, [5]float64{a.MinX, a.MinZ, a.MaxX, a.MaxZ, float64(a.Surf)})
	}
	for _, h := range b.Hangars {
		j.Hangars = append(j.Hangars, [4]float64{h.Spawn.X, h.Spawn.Y, h.Spawn.Z, round(h.Heading, 1e6)})
	}
	return j
}
```

```go
package sim

import (

	"testing"

	"playground/internal/geom"

)

var (
	runwayG = GroundSample{H: 10, Surf: maps.SurfRunway}
	taxiG   = GroundSample{H: 10, Surf: maps.SurfTaxi}
)

func onGround(speed float64) FlightState {
	return FlightState{Pos: geom.V(0, 10+GearHeight, 0), Rot: geom.Identity(), Vel: geom.V(0, 0, -speed), Gear: true, Ground: true}
}

func roll(fs FlightState, in Input, k Kind, g GroundSample, ticks int) FlightState {
	for range ticks {
		fs = StepFlight(fs, in, SpecOf(k), FlightMods{Ground: g})
	}
	return fs
}

func TestRotateSpeeds(t *testing.T) {
	want := map[Kind]float64{F16: 78, F15: 82, MiG29: 75, Su27: 85}
	for k, v := range want {
		if SpecOf(k).RotateSpeed != v {
			t.Fatalf("%v rotate %v", k, SpecOf(k).RotateSpeed)
		}
	}
}

func TestParkedStaysPut(t *testing.T) {
	fs := roll(onGround(0), Input{Gear: true}, F16, runwayG, 300)
	if !fs.Ground || fs.Pos != geom.V(0, 10+GearHeight, 0) || fs.Vel.Len() != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("parked plane moved: %+v", fs)
	}
}

func TestTakeoffRoll(t *testing.T) {
	fs := onGround(0)
	lift := -1
	for i := range 720 {
		fs = StepFlight(fs, Input{Throttle: 1, Pitch: 1}, SpecOf(F16), FlightMods{Ground: runwayG})
		if !fs.Ground {
			lift = i
			break
		}
	}
	if lift < 300 || lift > 700 {
		t.Fatalf("lift-off at tick %d, want 300..700", lift)
	}
	if d := -fs.Pos.Z; d < 250 || d > 900 {
		t.Fatalf("takeoff roll %v m", d)

	y0 := fs.Pos.Y

	}
}

func TestNoLiftoffWithoutPitchOrBelowRotate(t *testing.T) {

	if !fs.Ground || fs.Vel.Len() < SpecOf(F16).RotateSpeed {

func TestAirborneIgnoresGroundSampleAndWindDrifts(t *testing.T) {
	a := FlightState{Pos: geom.V(0, 1500, 0), Rot: geom.Identity(), Vel: geom.V(0, 0, -200), Throttle: 1}
	b, c := a, a

	}
}
```

`flight.go` (v1 işlem sırası korunur; yeni satırlar işaretli):

```go
type FlightState struct {
	Pos      geom.Vec3
	Rot      geom.Quat
	Vel      geom.Vec3 // air-relative; the ground track adds the wind
	Throttle float64
	Gear     bool // landing gear down
	Ground   bool // rolling on the wheels
}

type FlightMods struct {
	Turbo  bool
	Wind   geom.Vec3    // moves an airborne plane, never one on the ground
	Ground GroundSample // terrain under the plane at the start of the tick
}

func StepFlight(fs FlightState, in Input, s Spec, m FlightMods) FlightState {
	in = in.Clamp()
	fs.Throttle = in.Throttle
	speed := fs.Vel.Len()
	fs.Gear = gearState(fs, in, speed) // new
	if fs.Ground {                     // new
		return stepGround(fs, in, s, m, speed)
	}
	fwd := fs.Rot.Forward()

	k := s.Accel / (s.MaxSpeed * s.MaxSpeed)
	thrust := s.Accel * fs.Throttle
	if in.AB {
		thrust = k * s.MaxSpeedAB * s.MaxSpeedAB
	}
	if m.Turbo {
		thrust *= 1.69
	}
	if fs.Gear { // new: gear drag
		speed += (thrust - k*(1+GearDrag)*speed*speed - Gravity*fwd.Y) * Dt
	} else {
		speed += (thrust - k*speed*speed - Gravity*fwd.Y) * Dt
	}
	speed = math.Max(speed, 20)
	// … v1 lines unchanged from `auth := Authority(speed, s)` through `fs.Vel = dir.Scale(speed)` …
	if m.Wind == (geom.Vec3{}) { // new: keep the v1 expression bit-for-bit without wind
		fs.Pos = fs.Pos.Add(fs.Vel.Scale(Dt))
	} else {
		fs.Pos = fs.Pos.Add(fs.Vel.Add(m.Wind).Scale(Dt))
	}
	if fs.Pos.Y > Ceiling {
		fs.Pos.Y = Ceiling
		fs.Vel.Y = math.Min(fs.Vel.Y, 0)
	}
	if fs.Gear && m.Ground.Surf != maps.SurfNone && fs.Pos.Y <= m.Ground.H+GearHeight { // new: touchdown
		fs = settle(fs, m.Ground)
	}
	return fs
}
```

`ground.go`:

```go
package sim

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
)

// Ground model constants (spec §3.4). client/src/sim/ground.ts mirrors them.
const (
	GearHeight     = 2.5   // m: origin above the ground on the wheels
	GearDrag       = 0.8   // extra drag factor with the gear down
	GearMaxDeploy  = 140.0 // m/s: the gear cannot be lowered faster
	GearMaxSpeed   = 160.0 // m/s: airborne, the gear is forced up above this
	GroundThrust   = 0.3   // thrust scale while rolling
	RollDecel      = 0.4   // m/s²
	BrakeDecel     = 8.0   // m/s²
	SteerMax       = 0.7   // rad/s
	SteerRadius    = 20.0  // m: turn rate <= speed / SteerRadius
	SteerFadeSpeed = 80.0  // m/s: steering authority 1 → 0.15
	RotateRate     = 0.25  // rad/s nose-up at full pitch input
	MaxGroundPitch = 0.26  // rad
	LiftoffPitch   = 0.09  // rad
	MaxSinkRate    = 6.0   // m/s at touchdown
	MaxTouchBank   = 20 * math.Pi / 180
	TaxiMaxSpeed   = 30.0 // m/s on taxiways, apron and hangar floors
)

// GroundSample is the terrain under a plane: height and paved surface.
type GroundSample struct {
	H    float64
	Surf maps.Surface
}

// gearState applies the gear rules (spec §3.3) to the desired state in.Gear.
func gearState(fs FlightState, in Input, speed float64) bool {
	switch {
	case fs.Ground:
		return true
	case fs.Gear && speed > GearMaxSpeed:
		return false
	case !in.Gear:
		return false
	case !fs.Gear && speed <= GearMaxDeploy:
		return true
	}
	return fs.Gear
}

// YawPitch is the wings-level orientation with the given heading and nose pitch.
func YawPitch(heading, pitch float64) geom.Quat {
	return geom.AxisAngle(geom.V(0, 1, 0), heading).Mul(geom.AxisAngle(geom.V(1, 0, 0), pitch)).Norm()
}

func headingPitch(q geom.Quat) (float64, float64) {
	f := q.Forward()
	return math.Atan2(-f.X, -f.Z), math.Asin(max(-1, min(1, f.Y)))
}

// stepGround rolls a plane on its wheels (spec §3.4). in is already clamped.
func stepGround(fs FlightState, in Input, s Spec, m FlightMods, speed float64) FlightState {
	heading, pitch := headingPitch(fs.Rot)
	k := s.Accel / (s.MaxSpeed * s.MaxSpeed)
	thrust := s.Accel * fs.Throttle
	if in.AB {
		thrust = k * s.MaxSpeedAB * s.MaxSpeedAB
	}
	if m.Turbo {
		thrust *= 1.69
	}
	thrust *= GroundThrust
	decel := RollDecel
	if in.Brake {
		decel += BrakeDecel
	}
	speed = max(0, speed+(thrust-k*(1+GearDrag)*speed*speed-decel)*Dt)
	steer := max(-1, min(1, in.Yaw+in.Roll))
	heading -= steer * min(SteerMax, speed/SteerRadius) * max(0.15, 1-speed/SteerFadeSpeed) * Dt
	if speed >= s.RotateSpeed && in.Pitch > 0 {
		pitch = min(MaxGroundPitch, pitch+RotateRate*in.Pitch*Dt)
	} else {
		pitch = max(0, pitch-RotateRate*Dt)
	}
	fs.Rot = YawPitch(heading, pitch)
	if speed >= s.RotateSpeed && pitch >= LiftoffPitch { // lift-off
		fs.Ground = false
		fs.Vel = fs.Rot.Forward().Scale(speed)
		fs.Pos = fs.Pos.Add(fs.Vel.Scale(Dt))
		return fs
	}
	fs.Vel = geom.V(-math.Sin(heading), 0, -math.Cos(heading)).Scale(speed)
	fs.Pos = fs.Pos.Add(fs.Vel.Scale(Dt))
	fs.Pos.Y = m.Ground.H + GearHeight
	return fs
}

// settle puts an airborne plane on its wheels: wings level, nose clipped to
// LiftoffPitch/2 (no bounce), horizontal velocity.
func settle(fs FlightState, g GroundSample) FlightState {
	heading, pitch := headingPitch(fs.Rot)
	h := math.Sqrt(fs.Vel.X*fs.Vel.X + fs.Vel.Z*fs.Vel.Z)
	fs.Rot = YawPitch(heading, max(0, min(LiftoffPitch/2, pitch)))
	fs.Vel = geom.V(-math.Sin(heading), 0, -math.Cos(heading)).Scale(h)
	fs.Pos.Y = g.H + GearHeight
	fs.Ground = true
	return fs
}
```

`vectors_test.go` tip eklemeleri:

```go
type vecGround struct {
	H    float64 `json:"h"`
	Surf int     `json:"surf"`
}

type vecWind struct {
	Base [3]float64 `json:"base"`
	Gust float64    `json:"gust"`
	Tick int        `json:"tick"`
	W    [3]float64 `json:"w"`
}
// vecSpec:       RotateSpeed float64 `json:"rotateSpeed"`
// vecState:      Gear bool `json:"gear,omitempty"`; OnGround bool `json:"ground,omitempty"`
// vecInput:      G bool `json:"g,omitempty"`; BR bool `json:"br,omitempty"`
// vecCheckpoint: Gear bool `json:"gear,omitempty"`; Ground bool `json:"ground,omitempty"`
// vecScenario:   Wind *[3]float64 `json:"wind,omitempty"`; Ground *vecGround `json:"ground,omitempty"`
// vecFile:       Wind []vecWind `json:"wind,omitempty"`
```

`toVecSpec`/`spec()` `RotateSpeed`'i taşır. `runScenario`: başlangıç durumuna `Gear: sc.Start.Gear, Ground: sc.Start.OnGround`; her tick `mods := FlightMods{Turbo: sc.Turbo}`, `sc.Wind != nil` ise `mods.Wind = geom.V(w[0], w[1], w[2])`, `sc.Ground != nil` ise `mods.Ground = GroundSample{H: sc.Ground.H, Surf: maps.Surface(sc.Ground.Surf)}`; input'a `Gear: cur.G, Brake: cur.BR`; checkpoint'e `Gear: fs.Gear, Ground: fs.Ground`. `-update` dalında `f.Wind = windSamples()` doldurulur (`W` = `WindAt` sonucu).

`vectors_scenarios_test.go` (v1 `start`, `steady`, `vecScenarios` buraya taşınır; v1 listesi AYNEN kalır, sonuna eklenir; `Ticks` yalnız sıfırsa 300 yapılır):

```go
func groundStart(h, speed, th float64) vecState {
	return vecState{Pos: [3]float64{0, h + GearHeight, 0}, Rot: q4(geom.Identity()), Vel: [3]float64{0, 0, -speed}, Th: th, Gear: true, OnGround: true}
}

// v2 scenarios: ground handling, gear and wind (appended after the v1 ten).
func v2Scenarios() []vecScenario {
	runway := &vecGround{H: 10, Surf: int(maps.SurfRunway)}
	taxi := &vecGround{H: 10, Surf: int(maps.SurfTaxi)}
	approach := start(10+GearHeight+12, -0.04, 100, 0.3)
	approach.Gear = true
	fast := start(1500, 0, 150, 1)
	fast.Gear = true
	wind := [3]float64{6, 0, -4}
	return []vecScenario{
		{Name: "f16-taxi-turn", Kind: "f16", Ground: taxi, Start: groundStart(10, 0, 0), Ticks: 300,
			Inputs: []vecInput{{From: 0, Th: 0.15, G: true}, {From: 120, Th: 0.15, Y: 1, G: true}, {From: 240, BR: true, G: true}}},
		{Name: "f15-takeoff-roll", Kind: "f15", Ground: runway, Start: groundStart(10, 0, 0), Ticks: 600,
			Inputs: []vecInput{{From: 0, Th: 1, AB: true}, {From: 240, Th: 1, AB: true, P: 1}}},
		{Name: "mig29-landing", Kind: "mig29", Ground: runway, Start: approach, Ticks: 420,
			Inputs: []vecInput{{From: 0, Th: 0.3, G: true}, {From: 150, G: true, BR: true}}},
		{Name: "su27-brake-stop", Kind: "su27", Ground: runway, Start: groundStart(10, 60, 0), Ticks: 300,
			Inputs: []vecInput{{From: 0, BR: true, G: true}}},
		{Name: "f16-gear-forced-up", Kind: "f16", Start: fast, Ticks: 300,
			Inputs: []vecInput{{From: 0, Th: 1, AB: true, G: true}}},
		{Name: "f16-wind", Kind: "f16", Wind: &wind, Start: start(1500, 0, 200, 1), Ticks: 300, Inputs: steady(0.2, 0.3, 0, 1, false)},
	}
}

func windSamples() []vecWind {
	var out []vecWind
	for _, b := range []struct {
		base [3]float64
		gust float64
	}{{[3]float64{6, 0, -4}, 2}, {[3]float64{0, 0, 10}, 6}, {[3]float64{3, 0, 0}, 0}} {
		for _, tick := range []int{0, 1, 59, 600, 12345} {
			out = append(out, vecWind{Base: b.base, Gust: b.gust, Tick: tick})
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `ground.ts` (Go `ground.go`'nun satır satır portu; `Math.max/min/sqrt/sin/cos/atan2/asin`):

```ts
// Line-by-line port of internal/sim/ground.go (stepGround, settle, gearState).
import { add, qAxisAngle, qForward, qMul, qNorm, scale, v3, type Q } from "./vec.ts";
import type { FlightMods, FlightState, Spec, StickInput } from "./flight.ts";

export type GroundSample = { h: number; surf: number };

export const GEAR_HEIGHT = 2.5, GEAR_DRAG = 0.8, GEAR_MAX_DEPLOY = 140, GEAR_MAX_SPEED = 160, GROUND_THRUST = 0.3;
export const ROLL_DECEL = 0.4, BRAKE_DECEL = 8, STEER_MAX = 0.7, STEER_RADIUS = 20, STEER_FADE = 80;
export const ROTATE_RATE = 0.25, MAX_GROUND_PITCH = 0.26, LIFTOFF_PITCH = 0.09;
const DT = 1 / 60;

export function gearState(gear: boolean, ground: boolean, want: boolean, speed: number): boolean {
  if (ground) return true;
  if (gear && speed > GEAR_MAX_SPEED) return false;
  if (!want) return false;
  if (!gear && speed <= GEAR_MAX_DEPLOY) return true;
  return gear;
}

export function yawPitch(heading: number, pitch: number): Q {
  return qNorm(qMul(qAxisAngle(v3(0, 1, 0), heading), qAxisAngle(v3(1, 0, 0), pitch)));
}

function headingPitch(q: Q): [number, number] {
  const f = qForward(q);
  return [Math.atan2(-f.x, -f.z), Math.asin(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, f.y)))];
}

export function stepGround(fs: FlightState, inp: StickInput, s: Spec, m: FlightMods, speed0: number): FlightState {
  let [heading, pitch] = headingPitch(fs.rot);
  const k = s.accel / (s.maxSpeed * s.maxSpeed);
  let thrust = s.accel * fs.th;
  if (inp.ab) thrust = k * s.maxSpeedAB * s.maxSpeedAB;
  if (m.turbo) thrust *= 1.69;
  thrust *= GROUND_THRUST;
  let decel = ROLL_DECEL;
  if (inp.br) decel += BRAKE_DECEL;
  const speed = Math.max(0, speed0 + (thrust - k * (1 + GEAR_DRAG) * speed0 * speed0 - decel) * DT);
  const steer = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, inp.y + inp.r));
  heading -= steer * Math.min(STEER_MAX, speed / STEER_RADIUS) * Math.max(0.15, 1 - speed / STEER_FADE) * DT;
  const rotate = s.rotateSpeed ?? Infinity;
  if (speed >= rotate && inp.p > 0) pitch = Math.min(MAX_GROUND_PITCH, pitch + ROTATE_RATE * inp.p * DT);
  else pitch = Math.max(0, pitch - ROTATE_RATE * DT);
  const rot = yawPitch(heading, pitch);
  if (speed >= rotate && pitch >= LIFTOFF_PITCH) {
    const vel = scale(qForward(rot), speed);
    return { ...fs, rot, vel, pos: add(fs.pos, scale(vel, DT)), ground: false };
  }
  const vel = scale(v3(-Math.sin(heading), 0, -Math.cos(heading)), speed);
  const pos = add(fs.pos, scale(vel, DT));
  pos.y = (m.ground?.h ?? 0) + GEAR_HEIGHT;
  return { ...fs, rot, vel, pos, ground: true };
}

export function settle(fs: FlightState, g: GroundSample): FlightState {
  const [heading, pitch] = headingPitch(fs.rot);
  const h = Math.sqrt(fs.vel.x * fs.vel.x + fs.vel.z * fs.vel.z);
  const rot = yawPitch(heading, Math.max(0, Math.min(LIFTOFF_PITCH / 2, pitch)));
  const vel = scale(v3(-Math.sin(heading), 0, -Math.cos(heading)), h);
  return { ...fs, rot, vel, pos: { x: fs.pos.x, y: g.h + GEAR_HEIGHT, z: fs.pos.z }, ground: true };
}
```

`flight.ts` `stepFlight(fs, inp, s, m)`: mevcut gövde korunur; değişiklikler (Go ile aynı yerlerde):

```ts
  let speed = len(fs.vel);
  const gear = gearState(!!fs.gear, !!fs.ground, !!inp.g, speed);
  if (fs.ground) return stepGround({ ...fs, th, gear }, { ...inp, p: pitch, r: roll, y: yaw, th }, s, m, speed);
  let fwd = qForward(fs.rot);
  // … k, thrust, AB, turbo (m.turbo) …
  if (gear) speed += (thrust - k * (1 + GEAR_DRAG) * speed * speed - GRAVITY * fwd.y) * DT;
  else speed += (thrust - k * speed * speed - GRAVITY * fwd.y) * DT;
  // … unchanged through `const vel = scale(dir, speed);` …
  const w = m.wind;
  const pos = w && (w.x !== 0 || w.y !== 0 || w.z !== 0) ? add(fs.pos, scale(add(vel, w), DT)) : add(fs.pos, scale(vel, DT));
  if (pos.y > CEILING) { pos.y = CEILING; vel.y = Math.min(vel.y, 0); }
  const out: FlightState = { pos, rot, vel, th, gear, ground: false };
  const g = m.ground;
  return gear && g && g.surf !== 0 && pos.y <= g.h + GEAR_HEIGHT ? settle(out, g) : out;
```

`wind.ts` (Go `WindAt` portu; `norm`, `add`, `scale`, `v3`). Çağrı yerleri: `stepFlight(x, inp, spec, false)` → `stepFlight(x, inp, spec, { turbo: false })`; `predictor.ts`'de `turbo` parametresi `{ turbo }` olur (imza değişikliği Task 18'de).

`world.go`:

```go
type StartMode uint8

const (
	StartAir StartMode = iota + 1 // spawn at altitude (v1); the zero Config value means this too
	StartRunway                   // spawn parked in a hangar
)
// Config: Start StartMode // runway needs Map; zero = StartAir

func (w *World) groundAt(x, z float64) GroundSample {
	g := GroundSample{H: w.cfg.Terrain.Ground(x, z)}
	if w.cfg.Map != nil {
		g.Surf = w.cfg.Map.SurfaceAt(x, z)
	}
	return g
}

func (w *World) mods(p *Plane) FlightMods {
	return FlightMods{Turbo: w.tick < p.TurboUntil, Ground: w.groundAt(p.Pos.X, p.Pos.Z)}
}
```

`Step` döngüsünde: `in = Input{Throttle: p.Throttle}` → `in = Input{Throttle: p.Throttle, Gear: p.Gear}`; `StepFlight(..., FlightMods{Turbo: …})` → `StepFlight(p.FlightState, in, SpecOf(p.Kind), w.mods(p))`; hemen ardından `w.groundProtect(p)`. `Reseat`:

```go
	until, life, gp := p.ProtectUntil, p.Life, p.GroundProtect
	p.NextKind = kind
	w.spawn(p, &w.pending)
	p.ProtectUntil, p.Life, p.GroundProtect = until, life, gp
```

`protect.go`:

```go
package sim

const (
	LiftoffProtectTicks = 8 * 60 // protection after the wheels leave the ground
	FireProtectTicks    = 2 * 60 // a runway life that fires keeps at most this
)

// groundProtect keeps a runway spawn protected while it is on the ground;
// lift-off starts the LiftoffProtectTicks countdown.
func (w *World) groundProtect(p *Plane) {
	if !p.GroundProtect {
		return
	}
	if p.Ground {
		p.ProtectUntil = w.tick + LiftoffProtectTicks
		return
	}
	p.GroundProtect = false
}

// endProtection runs when p fires: an air spawn loses protection at once
// (v1), a runway spawn keeps at most FireProtectTicks.
func (w *World) endProtection(p *Plane) {
	if !p.RunwayStart {
		p.ProtectUntil = 0
		return
	}
	p.GroundProtect = false
	p.ProtectUntil = min(p.ProtectUntil, w.tick+FireProtectTicks)
}
```

`spawn.go` (v1 `spawn` gövdesi `spawnAir`'e taşınır; RNG tüketim sırası aynı kalır):

```go
const hangarClear = 25.0 // m: a live plane this close (horizontally) takes the hangar

func (w *World) spawn(p *Plane, ev *[]Event) {
	if w.cfg.Start == StartRunway && w.cfg.Map != nil {
		side := w.baseSide(p)
		if h, ok := w.freeHangar(p, side); ok {
			w.spawnParked(p, h, ev)
			return
		}
		c := w.cfg.Map.Bases[side].Center
		w.spawnAir(p, c.X, c.Z, ev)
		return
	}
	x, z := w.spawnPoint(p.Team)
	w.spawnAir(p, x, z, ev)
}

func (w *World) baseSide(p *Plane) int {
	switch p.Team {
	case TeamNATO:
		return 0
	case TeamSoviet:
		return 1
	}
	return int(p.ID % 2)
}

func (w *World) freeHangar(p *Plane, side int) (maps.Hangar, bool) {
	for i, h := range w.cfg.Map.Bases[side].Hangars {
		if w.hangarClosed(side, i) {
			continue
		}
		taken := false
		for _, id := range w.order {
			q := w.planes[id]
			if q.ID != p.ID && q.Alive && math.Hypot(q.Pos.X-h.Spawn.X, q.Pos.Z-h.Spawn.Z) < hangarClear {
				taken = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !taken {
			return h, true
		}
	}
	return maps.Hangar{}, false
}

// hangarClosed reports a destroyed hangar target (base attack, Task 27).
func (w *World) hangarClosed(side, i int) bool { return false }

// fresh is p's next life: full health and loadout, nothing else.
func (w *World) fresh(p *Plane) Plane {
	s := SpecOf(p.NextKind)
	return Plane{ID: p.ID, Kind: p.NextKind, NextKind: p.NextKind, Team: p.Team, HP: s.MaxHP, Alive: true,
		Missiles: s.Missiles, Flares: s.Flares, Life: p.Life + 1}
}

func (w *World) spawnAir(p *Plane, x, z float64, ev *[]Event) {
	pos := geom.V(x, math.Max(spawnAlt, w.cfg.Terrain.Ground(x, z)+spawnAGL), z)
	rot := geom.Identity()
	if toCenter := geom.V(-x, 0, -z); toCenter.Len() > 1e-6 {
		rot = geom.LookRotation(toCenter, geom.V(0, 1, 0))
	}
	*p = w.fresh(p)
	p.FlightState = FlightState{Pos: pos, Rot: rot, Vel: rot.Forward().Scale(spawnSpeed), Throttle: spawnThrott}
	p.ProtectUntil = w.tick + ProtectTicks
	w.prev[p.ID] = pos
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvSpawn, Plane: p.ID, Pos: pos, Vel: p.Vel})
}

func (w *World) spawnParked(p *Plane, h maps.Hangar, ev *[]Event) {
	pos := h.Spawn.Add(geom.V(0, GearHeight, 0))
	*p = w.fresh(p)
	p.FlightState = FlightState{Pos: pos, Rot: YawPitch(h.Heading, 0), Gear: true, Ground: true}
	p.RunwayStart, p.GroundProtect = true, true
	p.ProtectUntil = w.tick + LiftoffProtectTicks
	w.prev[p.ID] = pos
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvSpawn, Plane: p.ID, Pos: pos})
}
```

`cannon.go` ve `missile.go`: `p.ProtectUntil = 0` → `w.endProtection(p)`. `game/roster.go` `Pick`: `swappedLife` yorumu "Life of the life in which Pick used its instant respawn"; koşul `g.swappedLife[id] != pl.Life`, atama `g.swappedLife[id] = pl.Life`.

```go
package sim

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
)

// bank is the roll angle of q: 0 wings level, sign = right wing down.
func bank(q geom.Quat) float64 {
	r, u := q.Right(), q.Up()
	return math.Atan2(r.Y, u.Y)
}

// LandingOK judges a touchdown from the state of the tick before contact.
func LandingOK(before FlightState, surf maps.Surface) bool {
	if !(before.Vel.Y >= -MaxSinkRate) || math.Abs(bank(before.Rot)) > MaxTouchBank {
		return false
	}
	switch surf {
	case maps.SurfRunway:
		return true
	case maps.SurfTaxi:
		return math.Sqrt(before.Vel.X*before.Vel.X+before.Vel.Z*before.Vel.Z) <= TaxiMaxSpeed
	}
	return false
}

// landing kills a plane that touched down badly, rolled off the pavement or
// raced on a taxiway (crashes ignore shield and protection).
func (w *World) landing(p *Plane, before FlightState, ev *[]Event) {
	if !p.Ground {
		return
	}
	if !before.Ground {
		if !LandingOK(before, w.groundAt(before.Pos.X, before.Pos.Z).Surf) {
			w.kill(p, 0, WCrash, ev)
		}
		return
	}
	switch s := w.groundAt(p.Pos.X, p.Pos.Z).Surf; {
	case s == maps.SurfNone, s == maps.SurfTaxi && p.Vel.Len() > TaxiMaxSpeed:
		w.kill(p, 0, WCrash, ev)
	}
}
```

`world.go` `Step`: `w.prev[id] = p.Pos` satırından sonra `before := p.FlightState`, `StepFlight` sonrası `w.landing(p, before, &evs)`; `if !p.Alive { continue }` (silah/yenilenme adımlarından önce). `w.groundProtect(p)` landing'den sonra kalır.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `rearm.go`:

```go
package sim

const (
	RearmTicks    = 3 * 60 // stopped this long on the own base: full rearm and repair
	RearmMaxSpeed = 3.0    // m/s
)

func (w *World) needsRearm(p *Plane) bool {
	s := SpecOf(p.Kind)
	return p.HP < s.MaxHP || p.Missiles < s.Missiles || p.Flares < s.Flares || p.Heat > 0
}

// rearm counts ticks stopped on the own base (any base in FFA) and refills
// everything once RearmTicks pass.
func (w *World) rearm(p *Plane, ev *[]Event) {
	side := -1
	if w.cfg.Map != nil {
		side = w.cfg.Map.BaseAt(p.Pos.X, p.Pos.Z)
	}
	own := side >= 0 && (p.Team == TeamNone || side == w.baseSide(p))
	if !p.Ground || p.Vel.Len() >= RearmMaxSpeed || !own || !w.needsRearm(p) {
		p.RearmTicks = 0
		return
	}
	if p.RearmTicks++; p.RearmTicks < RearmTicks {
		return
	}
	s := SpecOf(p.Kind)
	p.RearmTicks = 0
	p.HP, p.Missiles, p.Flares = s.MaxHP, s.Missiles, s.Flares
	p.Heat, p.OverheatUntil = 0, 0
	p.MissileRegenAt, p.FlareRegenAt = 0, 0
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvRearm, Plane: p.ID, Pos: p.Pos})
}
```

Not: `p.Heat > 0` parktaki uçakta soğuyarak 0'a iner; ikmal sayacı o sırada ilerler, sorun değil.

- `maps.json`: `{"maps":[{"kind","seed","size","res","heights","map":MapInfo (bld boş),"points":[[x,z,surf,ground],…]}]}` — 4 tür × seed 1, tür başına 40 nokta.

```go
package protocol

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"playground/internal/maps"
)

var update = flag.Bool("update", false, "rewrite testdata/vectors/maps.json")

var mapVectorsPath = filepath.Join("..", "..", "testdata", "vectors", "maps.json")

type mapVec struct {
	Kind    string       `json:"kind"`
	Seed    int64        `json:"seed"`
	Size    float64      `json:"size"`
	Res     int          `json:"res"`
	Heights string       `json:"heights"`
	Map     MapInfo      `json:"map"`
	Points  [][4]float64 `json:"points"` // x, z, surf, ground
}

func buildMapVec(k maps.Kind) mapVec {
	m := maps.Build(k, 1)
	var info MapInfo
	if err := json.Unmarshal(MapJSON(m), &info); err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	info.Bld = [][6]float64{}
	q := m.Terrain.Encode()
	raw := make([]byte, 2*len(q))
	for i, v := range q {
		binary.LittleEndian.PutUint16(raw[2*i:], v)
	}
	v := mapVec{Kind: k.String(), Seed: 1, Size: 10000, Res: 129, Heights: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(raw), Map: info}
	local := [][2]float64{{0, 0}, {890, 20}, {0, 120}, {-700, 60}, {0, 170}, {-250, 225}, {0, 60}, {0, 300}, {950, 0}, {-1200, 0}}
	for _, b := range m.Bases {
		for _, uv := range local {
			p := b.World(uv[0], uv[1])
			v.Points = append(v.Points, [4]float64{p.X, p.Z, float64(m.SurfaceAt(p.X, p.Z)), m.Terrain.Ground(p.X, p.Z)})
		}
	}
	for i := range 20 {
		x, z := -3900+float64(i)*411.7, 3700-float64(i)*389.3
		v.Points = append(v.Points, [4]float64{x, z, float64(m.SurfaceAt(x, z)), m.Terrain.Ground(x, z)})
	}
	return v
}

func TestMapVectors(t *testing.T) {
	var want struct {
		Maps []mapVec `json:"maps"`
	}
	for _, k := range []maps.Kind{maps.Ada, maps.Sehir, maps.Col, maps.Dag} {
		want.Maps = append(want.Maps, buildMapVec(k))
	}
	if *update {
		b, err := json.Marshal(want)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(mapVectorsPath, append(b, '\n'), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(mapVectorsPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%v (generate with -update)", err)
	}
	b, _ := json.Marshal(want)
	if string(append(b, '\n')) != string(data) {
		t.Fatal("maps.json drifted from maps.Build (regenerate with -update)")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** `surface.ts`:

```ts
// Paved surfaces from welcome.map.bases; mirrors maps.Map.SurfaceAt.
import type { BaseInfo } from "../net/protocol.ts";
import { heightAt } from "../render/heightmap.ts";
import type { GroundSample } from "./ground.ts";

export const SURF_NONE = 0, SURF_RUNWAY = 1, SURF_TAXI = 2;

export function surfaceAt(bases: BaseInfo[], x: number, z: number): number {
  let best = SURF_NONE;
  for (const b of bases) {
    for (const a of b.areas) {
      if (x < a[0] || x > a[2] || z < a[1] || z > a[3]) continue;
      if (a[4] === SURF_RUNWAY) return SURF_RUNWAY;
      best = SURF_TAXI;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

/** terrain.Ground (water level 0) and the surface under (x, z). */
export function groundSample(heights: Uint16Array, size: number, res: number, bases: BaseInfo[], x: number, z: number): GroundSample {
  return { h: Math.max(heightAt(heights, size, res, x, z), 0), surf: surfaceAt(bases, x, z) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`env.ts`:

```ts
// Flight environment for prediction: ground under a point and the wind.
import type { FlightEnv } from "../predict/predictor.ts";
import { groundSample } from "../sim/surface.ts";
import { v3 } from "../sim/vec.ts";
import type { GameState } from "./state.ts";

const CALM = v3(0, 0, 0);

export const AIR_ENV: FlightEnv = { turbo: false, ground: () => ({ h: 0, surf: 0 }), wind: () => CALM };

export function flightEnv(s: GameState, turbo: boolean): FlightEnv {
  const t = s.terrain, m = s.map, h = s.heights;
  return {
    turbo,
    ground: (x, z) => (t && m && h ? groundSample(h, t.size, t.res, m.bases, x, z) : { h: 0, surf: 0 }),
    wind: () => CALM,
  };
}
```

`state.ts`: `heights: Uint16Array | null = null;` ve welcome'da `this.heights = decodeHeights(m.terrain.heights);` (`render/heightmap.ts`'den import). `predictor.ts`:

```ts
function mods(env: FlightEnv, fs: FlightState, tick: number): FlightMods {
  return { turbo: env.turbo, wind: env.wind(tick), ground: env.ground(fs.pos.x, fs.pos.z) };
}
  push(seq: number, inp: StickInput, tick: number, env: FlightEnv): void {
    this.pending.push({ seq, inp });
    if (this.pending.length > MAX_PENDING) this.pending.shift();
    this.phys = stepFlight(this.phys, inp, this.spec, mods(env, this.phys, tick));
  }
  reconcile(server: FlightState, ack: number, snapTick: number, env: FlightEnv): void {
    this.pending = this.pending.filter((p) => p.seq > ack);
    let corrected = server;
    let tick = snapTick;
    for (const p of this.pending) corrected = stepFlight(corrected, p.inp, this.spec, mods(env, corrected, ++tick));
    // … offset/rotOffset/teleport unchanged …
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `weather.go`:

```go
// Package weather is the room's weather table: lock-range scale and wind.
// Visuals live in the client (render/weather.ts).
package weather

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/rng"
)

type Kind uint8

const (
	Clear Kind = iota + 1
	Cloudy
	Fog
	Rain
	Storm
	Night
)

var names = [...]string{Clear: "acik", Cloudy: "bulutlu", Fog: "sisli", Rain: "yagmurlu", Storm: "firtina", Night: "gece"}

type Spec struct{ LockMul, WindSpeed, Gust float64 }

var specs = [...]Spec{Clear: {1, 0, 0}, Cloudy: {1, 3, 1}, Fog: {0.6, 0, 0}, Rain: {0.8, 6, 2}, Storm: {0.7, 10, 6}, Night: {1, 2, 0}}

func ParseKind(s string) (Kind, bool) {
	for k := Clear; k <= Night; k++ {
		if names[k] == s {
			return k, true
		}
	}
	return 0, false
}

func (k Kind) String() string {
	if k < Clear || k > Night {
		return names[Clear]
	}
	return names[k]
}

func (k Kind) Spec() Spec {
	if k < Clear || k > Night {
		return specs[Clear]
	}
	return specs[k]
}

// Wind is the steady wind of k for a room seed: horizontal, seed-chosen direction.
func Wind(k Kind, seed int64) geom.Vec3 {
	a := 2 * math.Pi * rng.Mix(seed^0x57494E44, 0)
	return geom.V(math.Cos(a), 0, math.Sin(a)).Scale(k.Spec().WindSpeed)
}
```

`sim/world.go`: `Config`'e `Wind geom.Vec3; Gust float64 // base wind and gust amplitude (weather)`; `World`'e `wind geom.Vec3`; `Step` başında `w.tick++`'tan sonra `w.wind = WindAt(w.cfg.Wind, w.cfg.Gust, w.tick)`; `mods` → `FlightMods{Turbo: …, Wind: w.wind, Ground: …}`. `game.go`: `Settings.Weather weather.Kind`; `New`'de `if s.Weather == 0 { s.Weather = weather.Clear }`, `ws := s.Weather.Spec()`, `Config{…, LockRangeMul: ws.LockMul, Wind: weather.Wind(s.Weather, s.Seed), Gust: ws.Gust}`. `welcome.go`: `WeatherJSON(st game.Settings) json.RawMessage` (`Kind: st.Weather.String(), Wind: r3(weather.Wind(st.Weather, st.Seed), 1000), Gust, LockMul`), `Static.Weather`, `NewWelcome` `Weather: st.Weather`.

```go
	dir, ok := avoidSolids(self, m)
	if !ok || dir.Y <= 0 {
		t.Fatalf("tower 250 m ahead: %v %v", dir, ok)
	}
	self.Pos.X += 300
	if _, ok := avoidSolids(self, m); ok {
		t.Fatal("no threat 300 m to the side")
	}
}
```

`tactics.go`:

```go
const (
	solidHorizon = 2.0  // s of path checked against buildings
	solidMargin  = 30.0 // m clearance kept from buildings and hangars
)

// avoidSolids pulls up when the next solidHorizon seconds of straight flight
// pass within solidMargin of a building or hangar.
func avoidSolids(self sim.Plane, m *maps.Map) (geom.Vec3, bool) {
	end := self.Pos.Add(self.Vel.Scale(solidHorizon))
	if _, _, hit := m.Solids.Sweep(self.Pos, end, solidMargin); !hit {
		return geom.Vec3{}, false
	}
	fwd := self.Rot.Forward()
	return geom.V(fwd.X, 0.7, fwd.Z).Norm(), true
}
```

Not: `Index.Sweep` `r ≤ maxGrow (10)` varsayar; `maxGrow`'u Task 7'deki `index.go`'da `30` yap (bu task'ta) ve `TestSweep*` testlerini tekrar çalıştır. `game.go`: `Game`'e `env *bot.Env` (`New`'de `&bot.Env{Terrain: t, Map: m, Mode: s.Mode}`), `Step`'te `b.Think(&snap, g.env)`. `balance_test.go`: `br.Think(&snap, &bot.Env{Terrain: t})`.

```go
func TestHeadingErrSign(t *testing.T) {

		t.Fatalf("target right: %v", e)
	}
	if e := headingErr(geom.V(0, 0, -1), geom.V(-1, 0, -1)); e >= 0 {
```

```go
package bot

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
	"playground/internal/sim"
)

type phase uint8

const (
	phAir phase = iota
	phTaxi
	phLineup
	phTakeoff
	phClimb
	phRTB
	phFinal
	phRollout
	phRearm
)

const (
	taxiSpeed = 12.0  // m/s on straight taxiways
	turnSpeed = 5.0   // m/s while turning hard
	wpReach   = 15.0  // m: next waypoint
	gearUpAGL = 50.0  // m
	climbAGL  = 400.0 // m: hand over to the air logic
	leadAhead = 150.0 // m: centerline lead point on the runway
)

func flat(v geom.Vec3) geom.Vec3 { return geom.V(v.X, 0, v.Z).Norm() }

func headingErr(fwd, to geom.Vec3) float64 {
	f, d := flat(fwd), flat(to)
	return math.Atan2(f.X*d.Z-f.Z*d.X, f.X*d.X+f.Z*d.Z)
}

// taxiInput steers toward target at the given ground speed.
func taxiInput(self sim.Plane, target geom.Vec3, speed float64) sim.Input {
	e := headingErr(self.Rot.Forward(), target.Sub(self.Pos))
	want := speed
	if math.Abs(e) > 0.5 {
		want = turnSpeed
	}
	v := self.Vel.Len()
	return sim.Input{Yaw: clamp(2 * e), Throttle: math.Max(0, math.Min(0.5, (want-v)*0.15)), Brake: v > want+3, Gear: true}
}

// ground flies the runway phases; handled=false hands the tick to the air logic.
func (b *Brain) ground(s *sim.Snapshot, self sim.Plane, env *Env) (sim.Input, bool) {
	if env.Map == nil {
		return sim.Input{}, false
	}
	if b.phase == phAir {
		if !self.Ground {
			return sim.Input{}, false
		}
		b.startTaxi(self, env.Map) // just spawned in a hangar
	}
	base := env.Map.Bases[b.side]
	switch b.phase {
	case phTaxi:
		if b.wp < len(b.route) && b.route[b.wp].Sub(self.Pos).Len() < wpReach {
			b.wp++
		}
		if b.wp >= len(b.route) {
			b.phase = phLineup
			break
		}
		return taxiInput(self, b.route[b.wp], taxiSpeed), true
	case phLineup:
		e := headingErr(self.Rot.Forward(), base.Axis.Scale(b.dir))
		if math.Abs(e) < 0.05 {
			b.phase = phTakeoff
			break
		}
		v := self.Vel.Len()
		return sim.Input{Yaw: clamp(3 * e), Throttle: math.Max(0, math.Min(0.3, (3-v)*0.2)), Gear: true}, true
	case phTakeoff:
		if !self.Ground {
			b.phase = phClimb
			break
		}
		u := self.Pos.Sub(base.Center).Dot(base.Axis)
		in := taxiInput(self, base.World(u+b.dir*leadAhead, 0), 0)
		in.Throttle, in.AB, in.Brake = 1, true, false
		if self.Vel.Len() >= sim.SpecOf(self.Kind).RotateSpeed {
			in.Pitch = 1
		}
		return in, true
	}
	if b.phase == phClimb {
		agl := self.Pos.Y - env.Terrain.Ground(self.Pos.X, self.Pos.Z)
		if agl > climbAGL {
			b.phase = phAir
			return sim.Input{}, false
		}
		aim := base.Axis.Scale(b.dir).Add(geom.V(0, 0.35, 0))
		p, r, y := Steer(self.Rot, aim)
		return sim.Input{Pitch: p, Roll: r, Yaw: y, Throttle: 1, AB: true, Gear: agl < gearUpAGL}, true
	}
	if b.phase == phTakeoff || b.phase == phLineup || b.phase == phTaxi {
		return b.ground(s, self, env) // phase changed above: run the new phase this tick
	}
	return sim.Input{}, false // RTB phases: Task 24
}

// startTaxi picks the base and hangar the plane sits in and its route out.
func (b *Brain) startTaxi(self sim.Plane, m *maps.Map) {
	b.side = max(0, m.BaseAt(self.Pos.X, self.Pos.Z))
	base := m.Bases[b.side]
	best, bestD := 0, math.Inf(1)
	for i, h := range base.Hangars {
		if d := h.Spawn.Sub(self.Pos).Len(); d < bestD {
			best, bestD = i, d
		}
	}
	b.route, b.wp, b.dir, b.phase = base.Waypoints(best), 0, 1, phTaxi
}
```

`brain.go`: `Brain`'e `phase phase; route []geom.Vec3; wp, side int; dir float64`. `Think` başında ölü/kayıp uçakta `b.phase = phAir` (mevcut sıfırlamaya ek). Canlıysa:

```go
	if in, ok := b.ground(s, self, env); ok {
		return in
	}
```

hava mantığından önce; hava `Input`'unda `Gear: false`.

```go
// Edit changes a plane in place. For tests and tools only; the game never calls it.
func (w *World) Edit(id ID, f func(p *Plane)) {
	if p, ok := w.planes[id]; ok {
		f(p)
		w.prev[id] = p.Pos
	}
}
```

`land.go`:

```go
package bot

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
	"playground/internal/sim"
)

const (
	rtbHPFrac     = 0.5
	rtbClear      = 2000.0 // m: no enemy this close
	approachDist  = 2500.0 // m before the threshold (stays inside the play area on every map)
	approachAGL   = 125.0  // ≈ glide · approachDist
	finalSpeed    = 100.0
	gearDist      = 3000.0
	flareAGL      = 12.0
	glide         = 0.05 // ≈ 2.9°
	finalLead     = 600.0
	reachApproach = 500.0
	turnoffU      = 700.0 // runway left ahead needed for a takeoff from the stop
)

func wantsRTB(self sim.Plane, planes []sim.Plane, bomber bool) bool {
	empty := self.Missiles == 0 && self.HP < rtbHPFrac*sim.SpecOf(self.Kind).MaxHP
	if bomber {
		empty = self.Bombs == 0
	}
	if !empty {
		return false
	}
	for _, o := range planes {
		if o.ID != self.ID && o.Alive && sim.Hostile(self.Team, o.Team, false) && o.Pos.Dist(self.Pos) < rtbClear {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// land flies home, lands on the own runway and waits for the rearm.
func (b *Brain) land(s *sim.Snapshot, self sim.Plane, env *Env) sim.Input {
	base := env.Map.Bases[b.side]
	th, _ := base.Threshold()
	elev := base.Center.Y
	agl := self.Pos.Y - (elev + sim.GearHeight)
	along := self.Pos.Sub(th).Dot(base.Axis)
	switch b.phase {
	case phRTB:
		p0 := th.Sub(base.Axis.Scale(approachDist))
		p0.Y = elev + approachAGL
		lateral := math.Abs(self.Pos.Sub(th).Dot(base.Inner))
		if geom.V(p0.X-self.Pos.X, 0, p0.Z-self.Pos.Z).Len() < reachApproach ||
			(along > -approachDist && along < -1200 && lateral < 300 && math.Abs(headingErr(self.Rot.Forward(), base.Axis)) < 0.5) {
			b.phase = phFinal
			return b.land(s, self, env)
		}
		return b.flyTo(self, p0.Sub(self.Pos), 130, false)
	case phFinal:
		if self.Ground {
			b.phase = phRollout
			return b.land(s, self, env)
		}
		aimAlong := math.Min(along+finalLead, 300)
		aim := th.Add(base.Axis.Scale(aimAlong))
		aim.Y = elev + sim.GearHeight + math.Max(0, -aimAlong)*glide
		dir := aim.Sub(self.Pos)
		if agl < flareAGL {
			dir = geom.V(base.Axis.X, -0.03, base.Axis.Z)
		}
		return b.flyTo(self, dir, finalSpeed, -along < gearDist)
	case phRollout:
		u := self.Pos.Sub(base.Center).Dot(base.Axis)
		in := taxiInput(self, base.World(u+leadAhead, 0), 0)
		in.Throttle, in.Brake = 0, true
		if self.Vel.Len() < 1 {
			b.phase = phRearm
		}
		return in
	}
	// phRearm: hold until full, then take off from here or taxi back via the far connector.
	if self.RearmTicks == 0 && !needsAmmo(self) {
		u := self.Pos.Sub(base.Center).Dot(base.Axis)
		if maps.RunwayHalfL-u >= turnoffU {
			b.phase, b.dir = phTakeoff, 1
		} else {
			b.route = []geom.Vec3{base.World(700, 60), base.World(700, 120), base.World(-700, 120), base.World(-700, 40), base.World(-700, 0)}
			b.wp, b.dir, b.phase = 0, 1, phTaxi
		}
	}
	return sim.Input{Brake: true, Gear: true}
}

func needsAmmo(p sim.Plane) bool {
	s := sim.SpecOf(p.Kind)
	return p.HP < s.MaxHP || p.Missiles < s.Missiles
}

// flyTo steers toward dir holding speed with the throttle.
func (b *Brain) flyTo(self sim.Plane, dir geom.Vec3, speed float64, gear bool) sim.Input {
	p, r, y := Steer(self.Rot, dir)
	th := math.Max(0, math.Min(1, 0.5+(speed-self.Vel.Len())*0.05))
	return sim.Input{Pitch: p, Roll: r, Yaw: y, Throttle: th, Gear: gear}
}
```

`brain.go` `Think`'te `b.ground(...)` çağrısından ÖNCE:

```go
	if env.Map != nil && !self.Ground {
		rtb := wantsRTB(self, s.Planes, false)
		switch {
		case b.phase == phAir && rtb:
			b.side, b.phase = b.homeSide(self, env.Map), phRTB
		case (b.phase == phRTB || b.phase == phFinal) && !rtb:
			b.phase = phAir // an enemy came close: fight
		}
	}
```

`homeSide`: takımlıda `TeamNATO → 0, TeamSoviet → 1`; FFA'da en yakın üs (`Bases[i].Center` uzaklığı). `ground()` içindeki `switch`'e `case phRTB, phFinal, phRollout, phRearm: return b.land(s, self, env), true` (Task 23'teki "RTB phases: Task 24" dönüşü kalkar). İniş her zaman `+u` yönüne, `Threshold()` noktasına yapılır.

```go
package game

import (
	"testing"

	"playground/internal/bot"
	"playground/internal/maps"
	"playground/internal/mode"
	"playground/internal/sim"
)

func TestRunwayStartParksEveryone(t *testing.T) {
	g := New(Settings{Mode: mode.Team, Size: 2, Difficulty: bot.Normal, Seed: 1, Map: maps.Ada, Start: sim.StartRunway})
	for _, p := range g.Snapshot().Planes {
		if !p.Ground {
			t.Fatalf("plane %d not parked", p.ID)
		}
	}
}

// 3v3 normal bots from the runway on the city map, 3 minutes.
func TestBotsRunwayMatch(t *testing.T) {
	if testing.Short() {
		t.Skip("long")
	}
	g := New(Settings{Mode: mode.Team, Size: 3, Difficulty: bot.Normal, Seed: 4, Map: maps.Sehir, Start: sim.StartRunway})
	flew := map[sim.ID]bool{}
	kills, crashes := 0, 0
	for tick := range 60 * 180 {
		for _, e := range g.Step(nil) {
			if e.Kind == sim.EvKill {
				if e.Other == 0 {
					crashes++
				} else {
					kills++
				}
			}
		}
		if tick < 60*60 {
			for _, p := range g.Snapshot().Planes {
				if p.Alive && !p.Ground && p.Pos.Y-g.Terrain().Ground(p.Pos.X, p.Pos.Z) > 100 {
					flew[p.ID] = true
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if len(flew) != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("airborne within 60 s: %d/6", len(flew))
	}
	if kills < 6 || float64(crashes)/6/3 >= 0.5 {
		t.Fatalf("kills %d crashes %d", kills, crashes)
	}
}
```

```go
package maps

type StructKind uint8

const (
	StructHangar StructKind = iota + 1
	StructFuel
	StructRadar
	StructAA
)

var structNames = [...]string{StructHangar: "hangar", StructFuel: "fuel", StructRadar: "radar", StructAA: "aa"}

func (k StructKind) String() string {
	if k < StructHangar || k > StructAA {
		return ""
	}
	return structNames[k]
}

type StructDef struct {
	Side, Index int
	Kind        StructKind
	Box         Box
	MaxHP       float64
	Hangar      int
}

// structLayout is spec §3.10: kind, center (u, v), size along u, height, size along v, HP, hangar slot.
var structLayout = [6]struct {
	kind          StructKind
	u, v, w, h, d float64
	hp            float64
	hangar        int
}{
	{StructHangar, -250, 225, 40, 12, 30, 400, 0},
	{StructHangar, 250, 225, 40, 12, 30, 400, 5},
	{StructFuel, 420, 170, 20, 14, 20, 250, -1},
	{StructFuel, 500, 170, 20, 14, 20, 250, -1},
	{StructRadar, -480, 180, 12, 18, 12, 300, -1},
	{StructAA, 0, 320, 16, 5, 16, 350, -1},
}

func structures(b Base) []StructDef {
	out := make([]StructDef, 0, len(structLayout))
	for i, l := range structLayout {
		p, q := b.World(l.u-l.w/2, l.v-l.d/2), b.World(l.u+l.w/2, l.v+l.d/2)
		box := Box{Min: geom.V(min(p.X, q.X), b.Center.Y, min(p.Z, q.Z)), Max: geom.V(max(p.X, q.X), b.Center.Y+l.h, max(p.Z, q.Z))}
		out = append(out, StructDef{Side: b.Side, Index: i, Kind: l.kind, Box: box, MaxHP: l.hp, Hangar: l.hangar})
	}
	return out
}
```

(`import "playground/internal/geom"`.) `Build` sonunda `for _, b := range m.Bases { m.Structures = append(m.Structures, structures(b)...) }`.

```go
package sim

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
)

const (
	StructIDBase    ID = 1 << 22 // below missiles (1<<24) and bombs (1<<25)
	StructCannonMul    = 0.25    // cannon damage scale against structures
)

func StructID(side, index int) ID { return StructIDBase + ID(side*8+index) }

func StructTeam(id ID) (Team, bool) {
	if id < StructIDBase || id >= StructIDBase+16 {
		return TeamNone, false
	}
	return Team((id-StructIDBase)/8 + 1), true
}

type Structure struct {
	ID    ID
	Side  int
	Kind  maps.StructKind
	HP    float64
	Alive bool
}

func (w *World) initStructures() {
	if !w.cfg.Structures || w.cfg.Map == nil {
		return
	}
	w.structs = make([]Structure, len(w.cfg.Map.Structures))
	w.resetStructures()
}

func (w *World) resetStructures() {
	for i, d := range w.cfg.Map.Structures {
		w.structs[i] = Structure{ID: StructID(d.Side, d.Index), Side: d.Side, Kind: d.Kind, HP: d.MaxHP, Alive: true}
	}
}

func structTeam(s Structure) Team { return Team(s.Side + 1) }

func (w *World) damageStruct(i int, by ID, dmg float64, weapon Weapon, at geom.Vec3, ev *[]Event) {
	s := &w.structs[i]
	if !s.Alive || !(dmg > 0) {
		return
	}
	s.HP = math.Max(0, s.HP-dmg)
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvStructHit, Plane: s.ID, Other: by, Value: dmg, Pos: at, Weapon: weapon})
	if s.HP == 0 {
		s.Alive = false
		*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvStructDown, Plane: s.ID, Other: by, Pos: at})
	}
}

// structSweep is the first live structure (filtered by keep) the segment a→b enters, boxes grown by r.
func (w *World) structSweep(a, b geom.Vec3, r float64, keep func(i int) bool) (float64, int, bool) {
	best, hit := math.Inf(1), -1
	g := geom.V(r, r, r)
	for i, s := range w.structs {
		if !s.Alive || !keep(i) {
			continue
		}
		bx := w.cfg.Map.Structures[i].Box
		if t, ok := maps.SegBox(a, b.Sub(a), bx.Min.Sub(g), bx.Max.Add(g)); ok && t < best {
			best, hit = t, i
		}
	}
	return best, hit, hit >= 0
}
```

(Task 7'deki `segBox`'ı `maps.SegBox` olarak dışa aç; `Index.Sweep` onu çağırır.) `World`'e `structs []Structure`; `NewWorld` sonunda `w.initStructures()`; `ResetAll`'da `if w.structs != nil { w.resetStructures() }`; `Snapshot`'ta `Structures: slices.Clone(w.structs)`. `hangarClosed`:

```go
func (w *World) hangarClosed(side, slot int) bool {
	for i, d := range w.cfg.Map.Structures {
		if w.structs != nil && d.Side == side && d.Hangar == slot && !w.structs[i].Alive {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
```

```go
		if ts, i, ok := w.structSweep(b.Pos, end, 0, hostile); ok && (hit == nil || ts < best) {
```

```go
package sim

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
)

const (
	BombRadius    = 40.0
	BombStructDmg = 260.0
	BombPlaneDmg  = 80.0
	BombCooldown  = 30 // ticks
	BombLife      = 30 * 60

	bombIDBase ID = 1 << 25
)

type Bomb struct {
	ID, Owner  ID
	Team       Team
	Pos, Vel   geom.Vec3
	ExpireTick int
}

func (w *World) dropBomb(p *Plane, in Input, ev *[]Event) {
	if !in.Bomb || p.Bombs <= 0 || w.tick < p.BombReadyTick {
		return
	}
	p.Bombs--
	p.BombReadyTick = w.tick + BombCooldown
	w.nextBomb++
	bm := &Bomb{ID: bombIDBase + w.nextBomb, Owner: p.ID, Team: p.Team,
		Pos: p.Pos.Sub(p.Rot.Up().Scale(2)), Vel: p.Vel.Add(geom.V(0, -2, 0)), ExpireTick: w.tick + BombLife}
	w.bombs = append(w.bombs, bm)
	w.endProtection(p)
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvBombDrop, Plane: bm.ID, By: p.ID, Pos: bm.Pos, Vel: bm.Vel})
}

func (w *World) stepBombs(ev *[]Event) {
	kept := w.bombs[:0]
	for _, bm := range w.bombs {
		start := bm.Pos
		bm.Vel.Y -= Gravity * Dt
		end := bm.Pos.Add(bm.Vel.Scale(Dt))
		at, hit := end, false
		if w.cfg.Map != nil {
			if t, _, ok := w.cfg.Map.Solids.Sweep(start, end, 0); ok {
				at, hit = start.Lerp(end, t), true
			}
		}
		if w.structs != nil {
			if t, _, ok := w.structSweep(start, end, 0, func(int) bool { return true }); ok && (!hit || start.Lerp(end, t).Dist(start) < at.Dist(start)) {
				at, hit = start.Lerp(end, t), true
			}
		}
		if g := w.cfg.Terrain.Ground(end.X, end.Z); !hit && end.Y <= g {
			at, hit = geom.V(end.X, g, end.Z), true
		}
		if hit || w.tick >= bm.ExpireTick {
			w.explode(bm, at, ev)
			continue
		}
		bm.Pos = end
		kept = append(kept, bm)
	}
	clear(w.bombs[len(kept):])
	w.bombs = kept
}

func boxDist(p geom.Vec3, b maps.Box) float64 {
	c := geom.V(math.Max(b.Min.X, math.Min(p.X, b.Max.X)), math.Max(b.Min.Y, math.Min(p.Y, b.Max.Y)), math.Max(b.Min.Z, math.Min(p.Z, b.Max.Z)))
	return c.Dist(p)
}

func (w *World) explode(bm *Bomb, at geom.Vec3, ev *[]Event) {
	*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvBombHit, Plane: bm.ID, By: bm.Owner, Pos: at})
	for i, s := range w.structs {
		if !s.Alive || !Hostile(bm.Team, structTeam(s), false) {
			continue
		}
		if d := boxDist(at, w.cfg.Map.Structures[i].Box); d < BombRadius {
			w.damageStruct(i, bm.Owner, BombStructDmg*(1-d/BombRadius), WBomb, at, ev)
		}
	}
	for _, id := range w.order {
		p := w.planes[id]
		if !p.Alive || p.ID == bm.Owner || !Hostile(bm.Team, p.Team, w.cfg.FriendlyFire) {
			continue
		}
		if d := p.Pos.Dist(at); d < BombRadius {
			w.damage(p, bm.Owner, BombPlaneDmg*(1-d/BombRadius), WBomb, ev)
		}
	}
}
```

`World`'e `bombs []*Bomb; nextBomb ID`; `Step`'te `w.dropFlare`'dan sonra `w.dropBomb(p, in, &evs)`, `w.stepMissiles` sonrası `w.stepBombs(&evs)`; `ResetAll`'da `w.bombs = nil`; `RemovePlane`'de `w.bombs = slices.DeleteFunc(w.bombs, func(b *Bomb) bool { return b.Owner == id })`; `Snapshot.Bombs` kopyası (`[]Bomb`). `fresh` `Bombs: w.cfg.Bombs`; `needsRearm`'a `|| p.Bombs < w.cfg.Bombs`, `rearm` dolumuna `p.Bombs = w.cfg.Bombs`. `types.go`: `Input.Bomb bool`, `Plane.Bombs, BombReadyTick int`, olaylar listenin sonuna `EvBombDrop, EvBombHit`.

```go
package sim

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
)

const (
	AARange      = 1500.0
	AAInterval   = 20 // ticks between shells
	AAShellSpeed = 450.0
	AASpread     = 0.015 // rad per axis
	AAShellLife  = 240
	AADmg        = 7.0
)

// stepAA fires each live AA site at the nearest hostile plane in range,
// leading it; shells are bullets owned by no plane.
func (w *World) stepAA(ev *[]Event) {
	for i, s := range w.structs {
		if !s.Alive || s.Kind != maps.StructAA || w.tick < w.aaReady[i] {
			continue
		}
		box := w.cfg.Map.Structures[i].Box
		gun := geom.V((box.Min.X+box.Max.X)/2, box.Max.Y+1, (box.Min.Z+box.Max.Z)/2)
		team := structTeam(s)
		var tgt *Plane
		best := AARange
		for _, id := range w.order {
			p := w.planes[id]
			if d := p.Pos.Dist(gun); p.Alive && Hostile(team, p.Team, false) && d < best {
				tgt, best = p, d
			}
		}
		if tgt == nil {
			continue
		}
		w.aaReady[i] = w.tick + AAInterval
		lead := tgt.Pos.Add(tgt.Vel.Scale(best / AAShellSpeed))
		dir := lead.Sub(gun).Norm()
		side := dir.Cross(geom.V(0, 1, 0)).Norm()
		up := side.Cross(dir)
		yaw := (w.rng.Float64()*2 - 1) * AASpread
		pitch := (w.rng.Float64()*2 - 1) * AASpread
		dir = geom.AxisAngle(up, yaw).Mul(geom.AxisAngle(side, pitch)).Rotate(dir)
		if math.IsNaN(dir.X) {
			continue
		}
		b := Bullet{Owner: 0, Team: team, Pos: gun, Vel: dir.Scale(AAShellSpeed), ExpireTick: w.tick + AAShellLife, Dmg: AADmg, Weapon: WAA}
		w.bullets = append(w.bullets, b)
		*ev = append(*ev, Event{Kind: EvFire, Plane: 0, Other: s.ID, Pos: b.Pos, Vel: b.Vel, Weapon: WAA})
	}
}
```

`initStructures`'da `w.aaReady = make([]int, len(w.structs))`; `Step`'te `if w.structs != nil { w.stepAA(&evs) }` `w.stepBullets`'tan önce.

```go
type baseRules struct{ perTeam int }

func (baseRules) Kind() Kind           { return Base }
func (r baseRules) Slots() int         { return 2 * r.perTeam }
func (baseRules) FriendlyFire() bool   { return false }
func (baseRules) KillLimit() int       { return 0 }
func (baseRules) DurationTicks() int   { return 10 * 60 * 60 }
func (baseRules) TeamFor(c map[sim.Team]int) sim.Team { return teamRules{}.TeamFor(c) }

// Over: a side whose targets are all down loses; time-up is the game's call.
func (baseRules) Over(b *Scoreboard) (bool, string) {
	switch {
	case !b.HasObjective():
		return false, ""
	case b.ObjectiveHP(sim.TeamNATO) <= 0:
		return true, "Sovyet"
	case b.ObjectiveHP(sim.TeamSoviet) <= 0:
		return true, "NATO"
	}
	return false, ""
}
```

```go
		if ev.Other != 0 {
			b.line(ev.Other).Score += 2
		}
		return
	case sim.EvKill:
	default:

	}
```

```go
// finish ends the round with the rules' raw winner: a team name, an FFA
// plane ID or the draw sentinel.
func (g *Game) finish(raw string) {
	g.winTeam, g.winID = sim.TeamNone, 0
	switch raw {
	case "NATO":
		g.winTeam = sim.TeamNATO
	case "Sovyet":
		g.winTeam = sim.TeamSoviet
	case drawWinner:
	default:
		if id, err := strconv.Atoi(raw); err == nil {
			g.winID = sim.ID(id)
		}
	}
	g.phase, g.winner = Ended, g.winnerName(raw)
	g.phaseEnd = g.tick + EndedTicks
}
```

```go
	s := newSession(1, nil)
	s.push(1, sim.Input{Bomb: true})

	s.queue, s.seqs = s.queue[:0], s.seqs[:0]
	if rep, _ := s.next(); rep.Bomb {

	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `bomber.go`:

```go
package bot

import (
	"math"

	"playground/internal/geom"
	"playground/internal/maps"
	"playground/internal/mode"
	"playground/internal/sim"
)

const (
	bombAGL     = 500.0 // m: level bombing run height
	bombRelease = 30.0  // m: release when the predicted impact is this close
)

func bombImpact(pos, vel geom.Vec3, groundY float64) geom.Vec3 {
	vy := vel.Y - 2
	h := pos.Y - groundY
	t := (vy + math.Sqrt(vy*vy+2*sim.Gravity*h)) / sim.Gravity
	return geom.V(pos.X+vel.X*t, groundY, pos.Z+vel.Z*t)
}

func nearestTarget(self sim.Plane, s *sim.Snapshot, m *maps.Map) (maps.StructDef, bool) {
	var best maps.StructDef
	bestD, found := math.Inf(1), false
	for i, st := range s.Structures {
		d := m.Structures[i]
		if !st.Alive || !sim.Hostile(self.Team, sim.Team(d.Side+1), false) {
			continue
		}
		c := d.Box.Min.Add(d.Box.Max).Scale(0.5)
		if dist := c.Dist(self.Pos); dist < bestD {
			best, bestD, found = d, dist, true
		}
	}
	return best, found
}

// bomberRole flies a level run at bombAGL over the nearest live enemy
// target and releases when the ballistic impact point is on it.
func (b *Brain) bomberRole(s *sim.Snapshot, self sim.Plane, env *Env) bool {
	if env.Mode != mode.Base || env.Map == nil || b.id%2 != 0 || self.Bombs <= 0 {
		return false
	}
	tg, ok := nearestTarget(self, s, env.Map)
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	c := tg.Box.Min.Add(tg.Box.Max).Scale(0.5)
	ground := env.Terrain.Ground(c.X, c.Z)
	to := geom.V(c.X-self.Pos.X, 0, c.Z-self.Pos.Z).Norm()
	b.aim = geom.V(to.X, math.Max(-0.3, math.Min(0.3, (ground+bombAGL-self.Pos.Y)/400)), to.Z)
	b.throttle = 0.8
	imp := bombImpact(self.Pos, self.Vel, ground)
	b.bomb = geom.V(imp.X-c.X, 0, imp.Z-c.Z).Len() < bombRelease
	return true
}
```

`brain.go`: `bomb bool` alanı; `decide`'da kaçış (evade) dalından sonra, toplama dalından önce `if b.bomberRole(s, self, env) { return }`; `Think` dönüşünde `Bomb: b.bomb` ve ardından `b.bomb = false`. RTB çağrısı `wantsRTB(self, s.Planes, env.Mode == mode.Base && b.id%2 == 0)`. `land.go` `needsAmmo(p)` → `needsAmmo(p, env)`: üs modunda `|| p.Bombs < 2` eklenir (bombacı ikmalde bombasını bekler).

```ts
export type Look = { fog: [number, number]; clouds: { count: number; min: number; max: number }; light: number;
  rain: number; lightning: boolean; night: boolean; zenith: string; horizon: string };
export const LOOKS: Record<WeatherKind, Look>;
```

```go
// settings builds room settings from a create message; missing optional
// fields take the spec §8 defaults (ada, acik, pist, acik).
func settings(m protocol.ClientMsg, now time.Time) (game.Settings, bool) {
	k, ok1 := mode.ParseKind(m.Mode)
	d, ok2 := bot.ParseDifficulty(m.Diff)
	mk, ok3 := orDefault(m.Map, "ada", maps.ParseKind)
	wk, ok4 := orDefault(m.Wx, "acik", weather.ParseKind)
	start, ok5 := orDefault(m.Start, "pist", parseStart)
	listed, ok6 := orDefault(m.Vis, "acik", parseVis)
	seed := m.Seed
	if seed == 0 {
		seed = now.UnixNano()
	}
	return game.Settings{Mode: k, Size: m.Size, Difficulty: d, Seed: seed, Map: mk, Weather: wk, Start: start, Listed: listed},
		ok1 && ok2 && ok3 && ok4 && ok5 && ok6
}

func orDefault[T any](s, def string, parse func(string) (T, bool)) (T, bool) {
	if s == "" {
		s = def
	}
	return parse(s)
}

func parseStart(s string) (sim.StartMode, bool) {
	switch s {
	case "pist":
		return sim.StartRunway, true
	case "hava":
		return sim.StartAir, true
	}
	return 0, false
}

func parseVis(s string) (bool, bool) { return s == "acik", s == "acik" || s == "ozel" }
```

`handshake()` içindeki çağrı `settings(m, s.o.Now())`. `pump` `case protocol.TIn, protocol.TPing, protocol.TChat:` (chat oda tarafında Task 44'te işlenir; o zamana dek oda bilinmeyen tipi yok sayar). `welcome.go`: `Start: map[sim.StartMode]string{sim.StartAir: "hava", sim.StartRunway: "pist"}[g.Settings().Start]`. TS: tipler; `socket.ts`'de `entry: Create | Join | Quick`, welcome'da `join`'e döner (değişmez).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `summary.go`:

```go
package room

import "playground/internal/game"

type Summary struct {
	Code, Mode, Map, Weather, Phase string
	Humans, Seats, LeftS            int
	Listed                          bool
	Seq                             int64
}

// publish stores a fresh summary; called by the actor only.
func (r *Room) publish() {
	st := r.game.Settings()
	rd := r.game.Round()
	phase := "playing"
	if rd.Phase == game.Ended {
		phase = "ended"
	}
	r.summary.Store(&Summary{Code: r.code, Mode: st.Mode.String(), Map: st.Map.String(), Weather: st.Weather.String(),
		Phase: phase, Humans: r.game.Humans(), Seats: r.seats, LeftS: rd.TicksLeft / TickRate, Listed: st.Listed, Seq: r.seq})
}

func (r *Room) Summary() Summary { return *r.summary.Load() }
```

`room.go`: `Room`'a `summary atomic.Pointer[Summary]; seats int; seq int64`; `New(code, s, o)` sonunda `r.seats = r.game.Players()` sayısı (tüm koltuklar), `r.seq = o.Seq`, `r.publish()`; `join`'de `req.reply <- …`'dan ÖNCE `r.publish()`; `leave` sonunda ve `tick`'te `tick%RoundEvery == 0` iken `r.publish()`. `lobby.go`: `Lobby`'ye `seq int64` (`Create`'te `l.mu` altında `l.seq++`), `build` `room.New(code, s, room.Options{Seq: seq})`;

```go
func (l *Lobby) rooms() []*room.Room {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]*room.Room, 0, len(l.rooms))
	for _, r := range l.rooms {
		if r != nil {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (l *Lobby) List() []room.Summary {
	var out []room.Summary
	for _, r := range l.rooms() {
		if s := r.Summary(); s.Listed {
			out = append(out, s)
		}
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b room.Summary) int {
		if c := cmp.Compare(b.Humans, a.Humans); c != 0 {
			return c
		}
		return cmp.Compare(b.Seq, a.Seq)
	})
	return out
}

func (l *Lobby) Quick() (*room.Room, bool) {
	for _, s := range l.List() {
		if s.Humans < s.Seats {
			if r, ok := l.Get(s.Code); ok {
				return r, true
			}
		}
	}
	return nil, false
}
```
