```go
package server

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

const roomsTTL = time.Second

type apiCache struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	at   time.Time
	body []byte
}

func (c *apiCache) get(now time.Time, ttl time.Duration, build func() []byte) []byte {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	if c.body == nil || now.Sub(c.at) >= ttl {
		c.body, c.at = build(), now
	}
	return c.body
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, body []byte) {
	h := w.Header()
	h.Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	h.Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	w.Write(body)
}

func errorJSON(msg string) []byte {
	b, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]string{"error": msg})
	return b
}

func (s *Server) apiAllow(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) bool {
	ip := clientIP(r, s.o.TrustProxy)
	if s.apis.Allow(limitKey(ip), s.o.Now()) {
		return true
	}
	s.rejects.note(s.keyedReason(s.apis, limitKey(ip), "api-rate"), ip.String())
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusTooManyRequests, errorJSON("çok fazla istek"))
	return false
}

type roomJSON struct {
	Code   string `json:"code"`
	Mode   string `json:"mode"`
	Map    string `json:"map"`
	Wx     string `json:"wx"`
	Humans int    `json:"humans"`
	Seats  int    `json:"seats"`
	Phase  string `json:"phase"`
	Left   int    `json:"left"`
}

func (s *Server) apiRooms(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if !s.apiAllow(w, r) {
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, s.roomsCache.get(s.o.Now(), roomsTTL, func() []byte {
		list := s.lobby.List()
		out := struct {
			Rooms []roomJSON `json:"rooms"`
		}{Rooms: make([]roomJSON, 0, len(list))}
		for _, x := range list {
			out.Rooms = append(out.Rooms, roomJSON{x.Code, x.Mode, x.Map, x.Weather, x.Humans, x.Seats, x.Phase, x.LeftS})
		}
		b, _ := json.Marshal(out)
		return b
	}))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `chat.go`:

```go
package room

import (
	"playground/internal/protocol"
	"playground/internal/sim"
)

const ChatCooldown = 2 * TickRate

func (r *Room) chat(from sim.ID, id int) {
	s, ok := r.sessions[from]
	if !ok || id < 1 || id > 6 || (s.chatAt != 0 && r.game.Tick()-s.chatAt < ChatCooldown) {
		return
	}
	s.chatAt = max(1, r.game.Tick())
	team := sim.TeamNone
	teams := map[sim.ID]sim.Team{}
	for _, p := range r.game.Players() {
		teams[p.ID] = p.Team
	}
	team = teams[from]
	msg := protocol.NewChat(from, id)
	for sid, o := range r.sessions {
		if team == sim.TeamNone || teams[sid] == team {
			o.out.Send(msg)
		}
	}
}
```

`room.go` `input` `switch`'ine `case protocol.TChat: r.chat(id, m.Chat)`. `typeOf` test yardımcısı `ChatMsg`'yi `"chat"` olarak tanımalı (`room_test.go`'daki `typeOf`'a dal ekle).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement:**

```go
// Package pilot issues and checks the anonymous pilot token. Only its
// SHA-256 is ever stored; the raw token is never logged.
package pilot

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/hex"
)

const (
	TokenLen = 22 // base64url of 16 bytes, unpadded
	Header   = "X-Pilot-Token"
)

func New() string {
	var b [16]byte
	rand.Read(b[:]) // never fails (crypto/rand panics instead)
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b[:])
}

func Valid(tok string) bool {
	if len(tok) != TokenLen {
		return false
	}
	for i := range len(tok) {
		c := tok[i]
		if !(c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z' || c >= 'a' && c <= 'z' || c >= '0' && c <= '9' || c == '-' || c == '_') {
			return false
		}
	}
	b, err := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(tok)
	return err == nil && len(b) == 16
}

func Hash(tok string) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(tok))
	return hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
}
```

```go
type Delta struct {

}

func (s *Store) Top(p Period, n int) ([]Entry, string) // entries, current week key
func (s *Store) Me(pilot string) (Pilot, bool)
func (s *Store) Dropped() uint64
func (s *Store) Close() error                    // final snapshot; idempotent
```

```go
func TestRecoverTruncatedJournal(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	c := &clock{t0}
	s := open(t, dir, c, Options{})
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Name: "A", Kills: 1})
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Kills: 1})
	s.crash() // power loss: journal flushed, no snapshot
	f, _ := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(dir, "pilots.jsonl"), os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	f.WriteString(`{"seq":3,"at":1,"d":{"p":"aa","k":`)
	f.Close()
	s = open(t, dir, c, Options{})
	if p, _ := s.Me("aa"); p.Kills != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("recovered %+v", p)
	}
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Kills: 1}) // written after the torn line
	s.crash()
	s = open(t, dir, c, Options{})
	defer s.Close()
	if p, _ := s.Me("aa"); p.Kills != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("the record after a torn line was lost: %+v", p)
	}
}

func TestNoDoubleCountAfterCrashBetweenRenameAndTruncate(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	c := &clock{t0}
	s := open(t, dir, c, Options{})
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Name: "A", Kills: 3})
	s.snapshotOnly() // snapshot renamed into place, journal not yet truncated
	s.crash()
	s = open(t, dir, c, Options{})
	defer s.Close()
	if p, _ := s.Me("aa"); p.Kills != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("double counted: %+v", p)
	}
}

func TestWeekRollover(t *testing.T) {
	c := &clock{t0}
	s := open(t, t.TempDir(), c, Options{})
	defer s.Close()
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Name: "A", Kills: 5})
	c.now = t0.Add(8 * 24 * time.Hour)
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "aa", Kills: 1})
	week, w := s.Top(Week, 20)
	all, _ := s.Top(All, 20)
	if w != "2026-W42" || len(week) != 1 || week[0].Kills != 1 || all[0].Kills != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("week %+v all %+v", week, all)
	}
}

func TestCapAndPrune(t *testing.T) {
	c := &clock{t0}
	s := open(t, t.TempDir(), c, Options{MaxPilots: 2, MaxIdle: 24 * time.Hour})
	defer s.Close()
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "old", Kills: 1})
	c.now = t0.Add(time.Hour)
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "mid", Kills: 1})
	c.now = t0.Add(2 * time.Hour)
	s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "new", Kills: 1})
	if _, ok := s.Me("old"); ok {
		t.Fatal("cap evicts the least recently seen")
	}
	c.now = t0.Add(25*time.Hour + 30*time.Minute) // cut-off t0+1h30: "mid" (t0+1h) goes, "new" (t0+2h) stays
	s.compactNow()
	if _, ok := s.Me("mid"); ok {
		t.Fatal("idle pilots are pruned at compaction")
	}
	if _, ok := s.Me("new"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("recent pilot kept")
	}
}

func TestRecordNeverBlocks(t *testing.T) {
	s := &Store{in: make(chan any, 1)}
	if !s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "a"}) || s.Record(Delta{Pilot: "b"}) || s.Dropped() != 1 {
		t.Fatal("a full buffer drops and counts")
	}
}
```

`store.go` (actor; tek kanal FIFO olduğu için `Record` ardından gelen `Me` her zaman o kaydı görür):

```go
// Package stats keeps per-pilot statistics on disk without a database: an
// append-only JSON-lines journal and a periodic snapshot, owned by one goroutine.
package stats

type Store struct {
	dir     string
	o       Options
	in      chan any
	done    chan struct{}
	dropped atomic.Uint64

	// owned by run()
	pilots  map[string]*Pilot
	seq     uint64
	journal *os.File
	w       *bufio.Writer
	size    int64
	compactedAt time.Time
}

type topReq struct {
	p     Period
	n     int
	reply chan topResp
}
type topResp struct {
	e    []Entry
	week string
}
type meReq struct {
	pilot string
	reply chan *Pilot
}
type ctlReq struct {
	op    string // "close" | "compact" | "snapshot" | "crash"
	reply chan error
}

func (o Options) withDefaults() Options {
	if o.Now == nil {
		o.Now = time.Now
	}
	if o.MaxPilots <= 0 {
		o.MaxPilots = 100_000
	}
	if o.MaxIdle <= 0 {
		o.MaxIdle = 180 * 24 * time.Hour
	}
	if o.CompactEvery <= 0 {
		o.CompactEvery = 10 * time.Minute
	}
	if o.MaxJournal <= 0 {
		o.MaxJournal = 8 << 20
	}
	if o.Buffer <= 0 {
		o.Buffer = 1024
	}
	return o
}

func Open(dir string, o Options) (*Store, error) {
	o = o.withDefaults()
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	s := &Store{dir: dir, o: o, in: make(chan any, o.Buffer), done: make(chan struct{}), pilots: map[string]*Pilot{}}
	if err := s.load(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	f, err := os.OpenFile(filepath.Join(dir, journalName), os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	st, err := f.Stat()
	if err != nil {
		f.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	s.journal, s.w, s.size, s.compactedAt = f, bufio.NewWriter(f), st.Size(), o.Now()
	if err := s.endLine(); err != nil { // a torn last line must not swallow the next record
		f.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	go s.run()
	return s, nil
}

func (s *Store) Record(d Delta) bool {
	select {
	case s.in <- d:
		return true
	default:
		s.dropped.Add(1)
		return false
	}
}

func (s *Store) Dropped() uint64 { return s.dropped.Load() }

func (s *Store) Top(p Period, n int) ([]Entry, string) {
	r := make(chan topResp, 1)
	select {
	case s.in <- topReq{p, n, r}:
		x := <-r
		return x.e, x.week
	case <-s.done:
		return nil, WeekKey(s.o.Now())
	}
}

func (s *Store) Me(pilot string) (Pilot, bool) {
	r := make(chan *Pilot, 1)
	select {
	case s.in <- meReq{pilot, r}:
		if p := <-r; p != nil {
			return *p, true
		}
	case <-s.done:
	}
	return Pilot{}, false
}

func (s *Store) ctl(op string) error {
	r := make(chan error, 1)
	select {
	case s.in <- ctlReq{op, r}:
		return <-r
	case <-s.done:
		return nil
	}
}

func (s *Store) Close() error  { return s.ctl("close") }
func (s *Store) compactNow()   { s.ctl("compact") }  // tests
func (s *Store) snapshotOnly() { s.ctl("snapshot") } // tests: snapshot without truncating the journal
func (s *Store) crash()        { s.ctl("crash") }    // tests: flush the journal and stop, no snapshot

func (s *Store) run() {
	defer close(s.done)
	tick := time.NewTicker(time.Minute)
	defer tick.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case m := <-s.in:
			if stop := s.handle(m); stop {
				return
			}
		case <-tick.C:
			if s.o.Now().Sub(s.compactedAt) >= s.o.CompactEvery || s.size > s.o.MaxJournal {
				s.logErr(s.compact())
			}
		}
	}
}

func (s *Store) handle(m any) (stop bool) {
	switch m := m.(type) {
	case Delta:
		s.apply(m, s.o.Now(), true)
		if s.size > s.o.MaxJournal {
			s.logErr(s.compact())
		}
	case topReq:
		e, wk := s.top(m.p, m.n)
		m.reply <- topResp{e, wk}
	case meReq:
		if p, ok := s.pilots[m.pilot]; ok {
			cp := *p
			cp.Kinds = maps.Clone(p.Kinds)
			m.reply <- &cp
		} else {
			m.reply <- nil
		}
	case ctlReq:
		var err error
		switch m.op {
		case "compact":
			err = s.compact()
		case "snapshot":
			err = s.writeSnapshot()
		case "crash":
			s.w.Flush()
			s.journal.Close()
			m.reply <- nil
			return true
		case "close":
			err = s.compact()
			s.journal.Close()
			m.reply <- err
			return true
		}
		m.reply <- err
	}
	return false
}

func (s *Store) logErr(err error) {
	if err != nil {
		slog.Error("stats", "err", err) // never logs pilot hashes or names
	}
}
```

`journal.go`:

```go
const (
	journalName = "pilots.jsonl"
	snapName    = "pilots.snap.json"
	maxLine     = 4096
)

type line struct {
	Seq uint64 `json:"seq"`
	At  int64  `json:"at"`
	D   Delta  `json:"d"`
}

type snapshot struct {
	V      int               `json:"v"`
	Seq    uint64            `json:"seq"`
	Pilots map[string]*Pilot `json:"pilots"`
}

// apply adds d to its pilot; journal=true appends it to the journal first.
func (s *Store) apply(d Delta, at time.Time, journal bool) {
	if d.Pilot == "" || d.Empty() && d.Name == "" {
		return
	}
	if journal {
		s.seq++
		b, err := json.Marshal(line{Seq: s.seq, At: at.Unix(), D: d})
		if err != nil || len(b) >= maxLine {
			s.dropped.Add(1)
			return
		}
		n, _ := s.w.Write(append(b, '\n'))
		s.size += int64(n)
		s.w.Flush()
	}
	p, ok := s.pilots[d.Pilot]
	if !ok {
		if len(s.pilots) >= s.o.MaxPilots {
			s.evictOldest()
		}
		p = &Pilot{}
		s.pilots[d.Pilot] = p
	}
	if d.Name != "" {
		p.Name = d.Name
	}
	p.Kills += d.Kills
	p.Deaths += d.Deaths
	p.Crashes += d.Crashes
	p.Wins += d.Wins
	p.Matches += d.Matches
	p.Fired += d.Fired
	p.Hits += d.Hits
	p.Flight += d.Flight
	for k, v := range d.Kinds {
		if p.Kinds == nil {
			p.Kinds = map[string]int{}
		}
		p.Kinds[k] += v
	}
	if wk := WeekKey(at); p.Week != wk {
		p.Week, p.WeekKills = wk, 0
	}
	p.WeekKills += d.Kills
	p.Seen = at.Unix()
}

// evictOldest deletes the least recently seen pilot (ties: smallest key).
func (s *Store) evictOldest() {
	var victim string
	var oldest int64
	for k, p := range s.pilots {
		if victim == "" || p.Seen < oldest || (p.Seen == oldest && k < victim) {
			victim, oldest = k, p.Seen
		}
	}
	delete(s.pilots, victim)
}

// endLine appends '\n' when the journal ends inside a torn line.
func (s *Store) endLine() error {
	if s.size == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	r, err := os.Open(filepath.Join(s.dir, journalName))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer r.Close()
	last := make([]byte, 1)
	if _, err := r.ReadAt(last, s.size-1); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if last[0] == '\n' {
		return nil
	}
	n, err := s.journal.Write([]byte{'\n'})
	s.size += int64(n)
	return err
}

// load reads the snapshot, then journal lines newer than it; a torn or bad line is skipped.
func (s *Store) load() error {
	if b, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(s.dir, snapName)); err == nil {
		var snap snapshot
		if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &snap); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("stats: snapshot: %w", err)
		}
		s.seq = snap.Seq
		if snap.Pilots != nil {
			s.pilots = snap.Pilots
		}
	} else if !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return err
	}
	f, err := os.Open(filepath.Join(s.dir, journalName))
	if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(f)
	sc.Buffer(make([]byte, maxLine), 64*maxLine)
	for sc.Scan() {
		var l line
		if json.Unmarshal(sc.Bytes(), &l) != nil || l.Seq <= s.seq {
			continue
		}
		s.apply(l.D, time.Unix(l.At, 0), false)
		s.seq = l.Seq
	}
	return nil
}

// writeSnapshot: temp file, fsync, rename, fsync the directory.
func (s *Store) writeSnapshot() error {
	if err := s.w.Flush(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	b, err := json.Marshal(snapshot{V: 1, Seq: s.seq, Pilots: s.pilots})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmp := filepath.Join(s.dir, snapName+".tmp")
	f, err := os.OpenFile(tmp, os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY|os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := f.Write(b); err != nil {
		f.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := f.Sync(); err != nil {
		f.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := f.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.Rename(tmp, filepath.Join(s.dir, snapName)); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	d, err := os.Open(s.dir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer d.Close()
	return d.Sync()
}

// compact prunes idle pilots, writes the snapshot and empties the journal.
func (s *Store) compact() error {
	cut := s.o.Now().Add(-s.o.MaxIdle).Unix()
	for k, p := range s.pilots {
		if p.Seen < cut {
			delete(s.pilots, k)
		}
	}
	if err := s.writeSnapshot(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := s.journal.Truncate(0); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.size, s.compactedAt = 0, s.o.Now()
	return nil
}
```

(`O_APPEND` ile açılan dosyada `Truncate(0)` sonrası yazımlar baştan başlar.) `query.go`: `Period`, `ParsePeriod`, `WeekKey` (`y, w := t.UTC().ISOWeek(); fmt.Sprintf("%d-W%02d", y, w)`), `Entry`, `Delta.Empty`, `Pilot.Favorite` (en çok tick, eşitlikte alfabetik ilk), `top(p, n)` (Week: yalnız `Week == WeekKey(now)` olanlar `WeekKills`'e göre; All: `Kills`'e göre; eşitlikte isim artan; `n`'de kes; boş isim `"Pilot"`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `stats.go`:

```go
package room

import (
	"playground/internal/game"
	"playground/internal/sim"
	"playground/internal/stats"
)

type StatsSink interface{ Record(stats.Delta) bool }

// counted reports the session of id when its pilot is counted.
func (r *Room) counted(id sim.ID) (*session, bool) {
	s, ok := r.sessions[id]
	return s, ok && s.pilot != "" && r.stats != nil
}

func (r *Room) countEvents(evs []sim.Event) {
	if r.stats == nil {
		return
	}
	teams := map[sim.ID]sim.Team{}
	for _, p := range r.game.Players() {
		teams[p.ID] = p.Team
	}
	for _, e := range evs {
		switch e.Kind {
		case sim.EvKill:
			if s, ok := r.counted(e.Plane); ok {
				s.tally.Deaths++
				if e.Other == 0 && (e.Weapon == sim.WCrash || e.Weapon == sim.WBounds) {
					s.tally.Crashes++
				}
			}
			if s, ok := r.counted(e.Other); ok && e.Other != e.Plane &&
				(teams[e.Other] == sim.TeamNone || teams[e.Other] != teams[e.Plane]) {
				s.tally.Kills++
			}
		case sim.EvMissileLaunch:
			if s, ok := r.counted(e.By); ok {
				s.tally.Fired++
			}
		case sim.EvHit:
			if s, ok := r.counted(e.Other); ok && e.Weapon == sim.WMissile {
				s.tally.Hits++
			}
		}
	}
}

func (r *Room) countFlight(snap sim.Snapshot) {
	if r.stats == nil {
		return
	}
	for _, p := range snap.Planes {
		if s, ok := r.counted(p.ID); ok && p.Alive && !p.Ground {
			s.tally.Flight += SnapEvery
			if s.tally.Kinds == nil {
				s.tally.Kinds = map[string]int{}
			}
			s.tally.Kinds[p.Kind.String()] += SnapEvery
		}
	}
}

func (r *Room) roundOver(rd game.Round) {
	teams := map[sim.ID]sim.Team{}
	for _, p := range r.game.Players() {
		teams[p.ID] = p.Team
	}
	for id, s := range r.sessions {
		if _, ok := r.counted(id); !ok {
			continue
		}
		s.tally.Matches++
		if (rd.WinnerTeam != sim.TeamNone && teams[id] == rd.WinnerTeam) || rd.WinnerID == id {
			s.tally.Wins++
		}
		r.flush(s)
	}
}

func (r *Room) flush(s *session) {
	if r.stats == nil || s.pilot == "" || s.tally.Empty() {
		return
	}
	s.tally.Pilot, s.tally.Name = s.pilot, s.name
	r.stats.Record(s.tally)
	s.tally = stats.Delta{}
}
```

(`Delta.Empty()` yalnız sayaçlara bakar; isim değişikliği tek başına kaydedilmez.) `room.go`: `Room`'a `stats StatsSink`; `New(code, s, o)` `r.stats = o.Stats`; `joinReq` `who Who`; `join` oturuma `pilot = who.Pilot`, `name` = roster'daki temiz isim; welcome `protocol.NewWelcome(id, r.code, r.game, r.static, who.NewToken)`; `tick`'te `evs` sonrası `r.countEvents(evs)`, snapshot alınan tick'te `r.countFlight(snapshot)` (aynı `g.Snapshot()` değeri yeniden kullanılır), `rd.Phase` `Playing`→`Ended` geçişinde (önceki `r.round.phase` ile karşılaştır) `r.roundOver(rd)`; `leave` ve `closeAll`'da `r.flush(s)`. `handshake.go`:

```go
	tok, fresh := m.Tok, ""
	if !pilot.Valid(tok) {
		tok = pilot.New()
		fresh = tok
	}
	who := room.Who{Name: name, Pilot: pilot.Hash(tok), NewToken: fresh}
```

ve tüm `rm.Join(ctx, name, p.conn)` / `r.Join(ctx, name, p.conn)` çağrıları `who` alır. Token hiçbir `slog` çağrısına girmez (`grep -n "tok" internal/server/*.go` ile kontrol et).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `api.go`:

```go
func (s *Server) apiLeaderboard(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if !s.apiAllow(w, r) {
		return
	}
	if s.o.Stats == nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, errorJSON("istatistik kapalı"))
		return
	}
	period, ok := stats.ParsePeriod(r.URL.Query().Get("period"))
	if !ok {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusBadRequest, errorJSON("geçersiz dönem"))
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, s.boards[period].get(s.o.Now(), boardTTL, func() []byte {
		top, week := s.o.Stats.Top(period, boardSize)
		if top == nil {
			top = []stats.Entry{}
		}
		b, _ := json.Marshal(struct {
			Period string        `json:"period"`
			Week   string        `json:"week"`
			Top    []stats.Entry `json:"top"`
		}{r.URL.Query().Get("period"), week, top})
		return b
	}))
}

func (s *Server) apiMe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if !s.apiAllow(w, r) {
		return
	}
	if s.o.Stats == nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, errorJSON("istatistik kapalı"))
		return
	}
	tok := r.Header.Get(pilot.Header)
	if !pilot.Valid(tok) { // checked before hashing: any length is refused in O(1)
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusNotFound, errorJSON("pilot yok"))
		return
	}
	p, ok := s.o.Stats.Me(pilot.Hash(tok))
	if !ok {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusNotFound, errorJSON("pilot yok"))
		return
	}
	b, _ := json.Marshal(struct {
		stats.Pilot
		Favorite string `json:"favorite"`
	}{p, p.Favorite()})
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, b)
}
```

(`stats.Pilot`'un `Week`/`Seen` alanları yanıtta zararsızdır; `Kinds` dahil.) `server.go`: `boards map[stats.Period]*apiCache{stats.Week: {}, stats.All: {}}`; rotalar `GET /api/leaderboard`, `GET /api/me`. `lobby.Options.Stats` → `room.Options{Seq, Stats}`. `config.go`: `fl.StringVar(&c.dataDir, "data", "", "directory for pilot stats (empty = stats off)")`. `main.go`:

```go

			slog.Error("stats disabled", "err", err) // the game runs without stats
		} else {
			st, sink = s, s
		}
	}
	lb := lobby.New(ctx, lobby.Options{MaxRooms: cfg.maxRooms, Stats: sink})
	h := server.New(lb, cfg.server(sub, st))
	// … in the shutdown goroutine, after lb.Wait(); h.Wait():
	if st != nil {
		if err := st.Close(); err != nil {
			slog.Error("stats close", "err", err)
		}
	}
```

(`sink` arayüzüne yalnız nil olmayan `*stats.Store` atanır; typed-nil tuzağı yok.)

```go
type Counter struct{ /* atomic.Uint64 */ }; func (c *Counter) Inc(); func (c *Counter) Add(n uint64); func (c *Counter) Load() uint64
type Gauge struct{ /* atomic.Int64 */ };    func (g *Gauge) Add(d int64); func (g *Gauge) Set(v int64); func (g *Gauge) Load() int64
type Histogram struct{ /* fixed bounds, atomic counts, sum */ }
func NewHistogram(bounds []float64) *Histogram; func (h *Histogram) Observe(v float64); func (h *Histogram) Quantile(q float64) float64 // bucket upper bound
func (h *Histogram) Count() uint64 // added by Task 50
type CounterVec struct{ /* fixed label set + "other" */ }
func NewCounterVec(label string, values ...string) *CounterVec; func (v *CounterVec) Inc(value string)
type Registry struct {
	Rooms, Humans, Bots, Conns                                  Gauge
	TickSeconds                                                 *Histogram // bounds 0.001 0.002 0.004 0.008 0.012 0.0167 0.025 0.05
	TickOverruns, MsgsIn, MsgsOut, SnapDrops                    Counter
	Rejects                                                     *CounterVec // reason (spec §11 set)
	API                                                         *CounterVec // path: rooms leaderboard me
	StatsDropped                                                func() uint64 // optional source, read at scrape
}
func New(statsDropped func() uint64) *Registry
func (r *Registry) WriteText(w io.Writer) error // + go_goroutines, go_memstats_heap_alloc_bytes
func (r *Registry) Summary() string              // "rooms=1 humans=2 bots=6 conns=2 tick_p99_ms=2.0 overruns=0 drops=0"
func Handler(r *Registry) http.Handler           // GET /metrics only; else 404
```

```go
func TestFetchPrintsBody(t *testing.T) {
	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) { w.Write([]byte("dogfight_rooms 1\n")) }))
	defer srv.Close()
	var out bytes.Buffer
	if code := fetch(srv.URL, &out); code != 0 || out.String() != "dogfight_rooms 1\n" {
		t.Fatalf("%d %q", code, out.String())
	}
	if code := fetch("http://127.0.0.1:1/none", &out); code != 1 {
		t.Fatal("unreachable must exit 1")
	}
}

func TestSummaryLoop(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	got := make(chan string, 4)
	go summaryLoop(ctx, metrics.New(nil), 10*time.Millisecond, func(s string) { got <- s })
	s := <-got
	cancel()
	if !strings.HasPrefix(s, "rooms=0") {
		t.Fatalf("%q", s)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**

### Task 61: Dokunmatik şema mantığı

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/input/touch.ts`, `client/src/input/touch.test.ts`
- Modify: `client/src/input/input.ts` (`InputState.touch`), `client/src/input/schemes.ts` (`Controls` `touch` içerir; `makeScheme` "touch" → `TouchScheme`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
```ts
export type TouchState = { stick: { x: number; y: number } | null; throttle: number; fire: boolean; brake: boolean;
  taps: Set<"missile" | "flare" | "bomb" | "ab" | "gear">; tilt: { x: number; y: number } | null };
export function newTouchState(): TouchState;
export const DEADZONE = 0.12;
export function stickVector(dx: number, dy: number, radius: number): { x: number; y: number }; // unit disk, deadzone → 0, screen-down → y<0
export function touchStick(s: { x: number; y: number } | null, rot: Q, invertY: boolean): { p: number; r: number; y: number };
export class TouchScheme implements Scheme { readonly kind = "touch" }
```
Sanal çubuk `p = y` (yukarı sürükleme = burun yukarı; `invertY` tersler), `r = x`; çubuk bırakılınca asist `steer(rot, yatay(fwd))`. AB ve TEKER dokunuşla aç/kapa (şema içinde durum), FÜZE/FLARE/BOMBA tek seferlik, ATEŞ/FREN basılı. Gaz `throttle` (kaydırıcı). Eğim açıksa ve çubuk boşsa `tilt` çubuk yerine kullanılır.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test** — `touch.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { qAxisAngle, qIdentity, v3 } from "../sim/vec.ts";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, makeScheme } from "./schemes.ts";
import { newTouchState, stickVector, touchStick } from "./touch.ts";

test("stick vector: unit disk, deadzone, screen y down", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(stickVector(5, 5, 100), { x: 0, y: 0 });
  const v = stickVector(0, -200, 100);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(v.x) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(v.y - 1) < 1e-9, "dragging up past the rim = full up");
  const d = stickVector(60, 80, 100);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.hypot(d.x, d.y) - 1) < 1e-9 && d.y < 0);
});

test("released stick levels the wings", () => {
  const banked = qAxisAngle(v3(0, 0, -1), 0.8); // rolled right
  const out = touchStick(null, banked, false);
  assert.ok(out.r < 0, `assist rolls left, got ${out.r}`);
  const held = touchStick({ x: 0, y: 1 }, qIdentity(), false);
  assert.equal(held.p, 1);
  assert.equal(touchStick({ x: 0, y: 1 }, qIdentity(), true).p, -1);
});

test("touch scheme: toggles persist, taps are one-shot", () => {
  const sc = makeScheme({ ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, scheme: "touch" });
  assert.equal(sc.kind, "touch");
  const touch = newTouchState();
  const st = { keys: new Set<string>(), buttons: 0, consumeMouse: () => ({ dx: 0, dy: 0 }), takePress: () => false, touch };
  const plane = { rot: qIdentity(), th: 0, gear: true };
  touch.throttle = 0.7;
  touch.taps.add("ab").add("missile").add("gear");
  touch.fire = true;
  let f = sc.frame(st, plane, 1 / 60);
  assert.deepEqual([f.stick.th, f.stick.ab, f.missile, f.fire, f.stick.g], [0.7, true, true, true, false]);
  f = sc.frame(st, plane, 1 / 60);
  assert.deepEqual([f.stick.ab, f.missile, f.stick.g], [true, false, false], "AB and gear stay toggled; missile was one tap");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `touch.ts`:

```ts
export function stickVector(dx: number, dy: number, radius: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  let x = dx / radius, y = -dy / radius;
  const l = Math.hypot(x, y);
  if (l < DEADZONE) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
  if (l > 1) { x /= l; y /= l; }
  return { x, y };
}

export function touchStick(s: { x: number; y: number } | null, rot: Q, invertY: boolean): { p: number; r: number; y: number } {
  if (!s || (s.x === 0 && s.y === 0)) {
    const f = qForward(rot);
    const flat = v3(f.x, 0, f.z);
    return len(flat) < 1e-6 ? { p: 0, r: 0, y: 0 } : steer(rot, flat);
  }
  return { p: invertY ? -s.y : s.y, r: s.x, y: 0 };
}
```

`TouchScheme`: `ab`, `gear` alanları (gear `reset` sonrası ilk karede `plane.gear`'dan); `frame` `st.touch` yoksa nötr döner; `taps` her karede okunup temizlenir (`taps.delete(...)`). `input.ts`: `touch = newTouchState()`; `clear()` çubuğu/ateşi/freni sıfırlar.
- [ ] **Step 4: Run, expect PASS.**

```ts
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base, alive: true }), { kind: "own" });
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base, dead: true, killer: 6 }), { kind: "follow", id: 6 });
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base, dead: true, killer: 2, deathAt: { x: 1, y: 2, z: 3 } }), { kind: "orbit", at: { x: 1, y: 2, z: 3 } });
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base }), { kind: "follow", id: 4 });
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base, spectate: 6 }), { kind: "follow", id: 6 });
  assert.deepEqual(camMode({ ...base, aliveIds: [] }), { kind: "overview" });
  assert.equal(watchingLabel("Viper"), "İzliyorsun: Viper");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `loop.ts`: `kill` kancasında kurban bensem `killer = killerId`, `deathAt = olay konumu`; doğunca sıfırla. Kamera: `follow` → `cam.update(dt, interp.pos, interp.rot, len(vel), false, null)` (klavye tarzı, uçağın yatışıyla); `orbit` → ölüm noktası çevresinde 0.3 rad/s, 120 m yarıçap, 40 m yukarı; `overview` → v1 `overview`. HUD: `follow`/`orbit` iken alt ortada `watchingLabel(isim)` (orbit'te "İzliyorsun: düştüğün yer"). `app.ts`: seçim bekleme ekranı kapalıyken (P ile kapatılmışsa) sol tık / → sonraki, ← önceki.

### Task 66: Son 10 saniye tekrarı

**Files:**
- Create: `client/src/game/replay.ts`, `client/src/game/replay.test.ts`
- Modify: `client/src/game/loop.ts` (snapshot'ları tampona yazar; oynatmada sahneyi tampondan çizer), `client/src/ui/app.ts` (R ile başlat/geç; `blocked()` oynatmada true), `client/src/ui/hud.ts` ("TEKRAR · R/Esc: geç", ölüyken "R: tekrarı izle")

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
```ts
export type RPlane = { id: number; k: string; tm: string; a: boolean; pos: V3; rot: Q };
export type RFrame = { t: number; planes: RPlane[]; missiles: { id: number; pos: V3 }[] }; // t = server ms
export class ReplayBuffer { constructor(spanMs?: number /* 10000 */); push(f: RFrame): void; frames(): RFrame[]; clear(): void }
export class ReplayPlayer { constructor(frames: RFrame[]); duration(): number; sample(elapsedMs: number): RFrame | null; done(elapsedMs: number): boolean }
```
Oynatma yalnız ölüyken başlatılır; `welcome.start === "hava"` iken kendi uçağım yeniden doğunca otomatik biter; `pist`'te park hâlindeyken sürebilir (spec §12.4, [K22]).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test** — `replay.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { ReplayBuffer, ReplayPlayer, type RFrame } from "./replay.ts";

const frame = (t: number, x: number): RFrame => ({ t, planes: [{ id: 1, k: "f16", tm: "nato", a: true, pos: { x, y: 0, z: 0 }, rot: { w: 1, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 } }], missiles: [] });

test("buffer keeps the last 10 s", () => {
  const b = new ReplayBuffer(10000);
  for (let t = 0; t <= 20000; t += 100) b.push(frame(t, t));
  const f = b.frames();
  assert.equal(f[0].t, 10000);
  assert.equal(f.at(-1)!.t, 20000);
});

test("player interpolates and ends", () => {
  const p = new ReplayPlayer([frame(1000, 0), frame(1100, 10), frame(1200, 30)]);
  assert.equal(p.duration(), 200);
  assert.equal(p.sample(50)!.planes[0].pos.x, 5);
  assert.equal(p.sample(150)!.planes[0].pos.x, 20);
  assert.ok(!p.done(199) && p.done(200));
  assert.equal(new ReplayPlayer([]).sample(0), null);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, expect FAIL.**
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `ReplayBuffer.push` sonda `t − spanMs`'ten eskileri atar (dizi başından; 30 Hz × 10 sn ≈ 300 kare). `sample`: `t0 + elapsed` için iki kare arasında konum lerp, yönelim `qSlerp`; bir karede olmayan uçak o anda gizlenir. `loop.ts`: her snapshot'ta `buffer.push({ t: ServerClock.serverMs(state.tick), planes, missiles })`; `replay` aktifken `views.sync` `planeRenders` yerine oynatıcı karesinden kurulan `PlaneRender` haritasıyla çağrılır (kendi kaydedilen uçağım `isMe`), kamera kendi kaydedilen uçağımı izler; `done` → normal görünüme dön. `app.ts`: ölü ve oynatma yokken `KeyR` → başlat; oynatmada `KeyR`/`Escape` → geç; `blocked()` oynatmada `true` (input nötr; pist modunda park hâlindeki uçak güvende).
- [ ] **Step 4: Run, expect PASS.**
- [ ] **Step 5: Verify** — client doğrulama komutu; botlu odada öl → R → son 10 sn oynar, R ile geçilir.

### Task 67: Compose `/data` hacmi, metrik portu, imajda `/data`, README

**Files:**
- Modify: `deploy/compose.yml`, `Dockerfile.runtime`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:** Consumes `-data`, `-metrics-addr`, `-get` (Tasks 48, 52). Produces: üretim komut satırı `-data=/data -metrics-addr=:9090`; kalıcı adlı hacim `dogfight-data` (sabit `name:` — sürüm başına compose dosyası ve proje adı değişse de aynı hacim).

- [ ] **Step 1: compose.yml** — `command`'a `- -data=/data` ve `- -metrics-addr=:9090 # internal only: not published, not proxied`; servis altına:

```yaml
    volumes:
      - dogfight-data:/data # pilot stats; the root FS stays read-only
```

dosya sonuna:

```yaml
volumes:
  dogfight-data:
    name: dogfight-data # fixed: every version's compose file shares it
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Dockerfile.runtime** — `COPY --chmod=0755 …` satırından sonra:

```dockerfile
# /data owned by the runtime user; a fresh named volume copies this ownership on first mount.
COPY --from=gcr.io/distroless/static-debian12:nonroot@sha256:afa5c872c891853ca7fcf1f12c3edb23f7eeef36189728842dd51042ff57f7ab --chown=65532:65532 /home/nonroot /data
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Verify** — Docker yerelde varsa: `VERSION=test TRUST_PROXY=172.18.0.0/16 docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml config >/dev/null && echo ok`; yoksa YAML'ı `node -e` ile ayrıştırmak yerine gözle kontrol et ve raporda belirt. `go build ./... && go test ./... -race -short` (değişmeyen kod, regresyon kontrolü).
